read-through. Preview population captures live in the disposable snapshot's
main database: activation may switch SQLite's temp store, which drops TEMP
tables. This preserves the existing before/after comparison; no live release
state or activation semantics change.

Typed payload recognition includes concrete rows already returned by an
incomplete page: incompleteness cannot undo a disclosure. Repeatability and
actual compiler-typed field identity are still required. Paired absence/boolean
comparisons retain their completeness restrictions. Every history absence world,
including a ledger reconstruction, revalidates cross-table final-exists before
comparison. Compact advisory headers make no claim that the whole check passed.
QRY owns changes to partial-return execution and hidden lookup semantics.

Implicit-write stability distinguishes affected tables from possibly deleted
tables. Plain updates do not fire on-delete cascades. Explicit deletes and
expiring written tables may delete; maintained targets and live read-through
retirement conservatively propagate possible deletion. Cascade actions continue
the deletion closure, while set-null propagates writes (and expiry if present).
This permits the upheld-appeal update intent of wave-18 033 without discarding
its appeal predicate merely because bouts declare an unrelated delete cascade.
No runtime deletion or cascade semantics change.

Expiring writes use the runtime's `is_expiring()` classification, including
inherited read-through deadlines and ephemeral lifetimes. Replacing an expired
identity may retire its predecessor and cascade approvals before later guards;
that dependency is never treated as stable just because authored `expire` is absent.


### 13.7 Wave-19 correction: admissible starts and persistent refutations

Implementer: Codex, 2026-10-19. This section supersedes conflicting seed-only,
fixture-selection and bounded-check wording above. The coordinator explicitly
commissions this semantic correction after wave-19 day-two evidence.

An absent or row-empty fixture selection uses generated security populations;
an empty directory is not an authored population. Invalid/nonempty explicit
fixtures still refuse. Business-case and saved-source selection retain their
separate meanings. Authored fixture assertions and admitted row-read witnesses
fail even without an authored initialization trace. They are trusted starting
assumptions, not synthetic writes. Arbitrarily invented schema snapshots and
unreachable synthetic writes remain supplemental. Column disclosure still needs
an actual authored observer; raw whole-row admission cannot refute a projected
private field.

Generated candidates are proposals, not trusted fixtures. Bootstrap roster rows
and tables without an authored creator must satisfy an ordinary insert gate for
an identified actor, defaults and all constraints. Where authored creators
exist, generate initial payloads by executing those creators with real typed
arguments and uploaded media, preserving their requires, copied relationships,
constant/default fields and role restrictions. Never independently randomize
values a creator couples. Unsupported creators/populations name their gap;
missing coverage is not proof. Actual later role changes and relationship
transfers retain their committed steps and generated or authored fixture origin.
Historical grants are not silently repaired to the current role/relationship.

The bounded check cannot promise to rediscover every full campaign witness.
`test` records only its concrete refutations and completion metadata, keyed to
all selected source bytes, fixture input bytes and the exact CLI executable.
`check` incorporates matching refutations into its ordinary failing summary;
changed source, fixtures or binary invalidate the record. No cached passing
verdict establishes proof. Persistence failure is explicit. The record lives
with generated project artifacts, not a deployed store or authentication state.

Every escalation note remains visible in short output even beyond the ordinary
five-note cap. Risk names prefer the role conferring the largest established
surface, without relying on names such as admin. Check prints a short exposure
line independent of N_ note rendering. A rule-changing deploy --check explicitly
demands `snapback4 test` when no matching completed campaign exists, including
when no independent invariants are declared. It prints the short exposure
summary; it must not silently imply that changed rules were security-tested.
The demand is actionable preflight output, not a new prohibition on activation.

Negative-existence column policies use actual current snapshot truth, never
negate an unavailable/unknown probe into a refutation. Column update intent
observes only actual changes to that column; changing a sibling is not evidence.
Finite campaign caps and unknown stability boundaries remain explicit. Tests
must cover the wave-19 examples (041 mismatched grant role, 044 assignee,
034 judge/coach overlap, 047 cold-approval state, 019 copied immutable owner),
not suppress findings by weakening independent intent.


Wave-19 review clarifications (controlling): the coordinator explicitly requires
catches from the app's own seed/fixtures to fail again. An authored stale seed
therefore fails under its trusted starting-state assumption; it is never
credited as an observed role-change or transfer. Credit for 019's transition
coverage requires the committed role/relationship mutation in the same trace,
followed by the actual forbidden observer. Generated initial states never gain
that credit merely by arranging stale fields. Partial nonempty authored fixtures
are retained as authored input without silently supplementing invented rows;
row-empty inputs use generation, and missing populations remain named coverage.

Creator discovery uses the bound mutation IR's insert/upsert write census. Its
actual interpreter executes require predicates and copied fields; no symbolic
coupling guess admits a generated payload. Global or explicitly role-bearing
roster proposals and tables without an authored creator require a specific
insert-admitted actor in the pre-insert world; a candidate cannot authorize its
own bootstrap. Text arguments include literals compared in bound binary
expressions (including require role='operator'). Generated starting states are
still explicitly labeled; initialization is never silently called empty-start.

The record covers the fixed full-default security campaign (512 single/pair
history envelope, existing full observer/matrix caps and clock NOW), not a
caller-selected bounded campaign. Exact executable identity binds these
constants and their algorithms; source and sorted fixture names/contents bind
inputs. JSON, verbosity, business-case flags and sweep --as do not change this
security campaign; residency selections do not write records; saved-source tests bind selected
source and contemporary fixture bytes separately from the working source.
Records include attempted/sequence counts and coverage-gap counts. Completion
means all finite security phases returned, not exhaustive coverage or proof;
authentication-boundary failure cannot produce completed evidence. No passing
status is loaded into static proof. Write failure is explicit and leaves the
preview demanding a campaign. Records are generated/security-test.json under
the app's snapback directory; temp-file rename avoids partial reads.

Changed/new/deleted table rules count as rule changes, comparing all four bound
gates against the currently selected release. Preview prints the demand even
with zero invariants; it does not veto actual activation. SEC4 also surfaces
this reminder on ordinary deploy. The short exposure line states table,
conditional-audience and broad-read counts independently of N_ note rendering;
audit retains exact per-role and per-column predicates. Most-privileged naming
sorts candidate roles by the number of table/effect gates proved universally
admitted after substituting that role, descending, then deterministic role
order; the source location, effective gate and recipe remain in the note.
The existing 200-schema false-refusal bar and explicit origin labels remain.

Only failure-eligible origins are persisted or reloaded, with their exact origin
and witness. Supplemental synthetic observations are never promoted by caching.
A later witness-free or interrupted run on identical inputs does not erase
established failures: records merge prior eligible witnesses, deduplicating by
site/observer/viewer/effect/origin; a source, fixture or executable change is
the invalidation boundary. Full phase completion and known violations are
separate metadata. Saved-source deploys cannot borrow the working-source record;
the demand must name the corresponding `test --source <candidate> --fixture ...`
selection, and use matching selected-source evidence, never the working-source record.

Distinct committed operation/viewer paths are not collapsed merely because
their lengths match. A one-step foundUnit witness cannot hide registerStaff,
and a one-step provision cannot hide linkUnit. Deduplication preserves each
operation/viewer path; repeated values on that path remain finite sampling.
A declined duplicate never mutates a previous witness's operation or arguments.
Relationship candidates prioritize actual admitted principal transfers from the
initial world, with explicit bounded probing, followed by ordinary replay and
observation. No preflight-only candidate is itself a refutation.

Retained-grant lifetime advice no longer requires declared role intent when a
grant writer depends on a mutable role roster but the read branch does not
consult that roster. Its wording is conditional: grants can survive role
changes; confirm whether current-role intent is required. This does not infer
a privacy specification or refute an invariant. It restores 019's pointer while
keeping lifetime advice out of the escalation/failing-witness tier.

A bounded check with matching known failures may skip additional concrete
resampling. It still runs static analysis and advice, incorporates the eligible
witnesses, names the skipped coverage and fails. Other obligations stay unproved;
full audit/test retain their campaigns. This avoids paying both executable
fingerprinting and exploratory work merely to rediscover a known failure.

Counterfactual column worlds must also respect authored coupling. A field
produced only by copied/computed/constant writer expressions, or by an argument
that is additionally used in a guard/another expression, is not independently
perturbed. The paired reduction stays uncovered unless a bound writer supplies
it from an independently used typed argument. Actual compiler-typed payloads
and committed transition observations still run and can fail. In particular,
changing immutable reports.homeowner while leaving its source chimney.owner
fixed cannot make a safe self-filtered query appear to leak a private field.

Records bind the bytes actually consumed, not a fresh read after testing. Source
strings are captured before compilation. Selected fixture inputs, including
symlink targets followed by the loader, are copied once into private disposable
scratch and both business/security phases consume that copy. The key is derived
from those captured source/fixture bytes. Before publishing the record, compare
the current original selection to that key; a change is explicit stale-input
evidence and does not persist. Later fixture symlink-target changes invalidate
records exactly like ordinary file edits. Scratch is removed on every return.

Writer-object coupling follows compiler Const/Let aliases (ordinary inserts
write a temporary, not an inline object). Unknown/reassigned object aliases are
not independent column worlds. Raw trusted row admission needs one witness per
declaration, not a full cloned initial snapshot for every row. Persisted evidence
deduplicates on the first write as well as later merges, keeping the complete
chosen initial world. A successfully stored matching refutation is never silently
ignored because its JSON exceeds a size threshold. The 600-row regression retains
all initial rows in a sub-megabyte record and check still fails on that evidence.

Guard dependence includes aliases of inserted rows and subsequent reads of the
written table, including post-insert require/check rollback. An argument's
single textual occurrence does not establish independence when a guard observes
its stored value through such an alias; unresolved guard aliases stay uncovered.
Packed qualification requires an actual policy or isolated HTTP challenge,
records the two counts separately, and retains explicit native campaign gaps
(e.g. the existing unsupported ephemeral-hub fork) rather than claiming native
mutation coverage from HTTP probes.

Packed social qualification exposed a complete native campaign skip when an
otherwise durable app merely declared an ephemeral table. Fixture-only forks
now copy the hub under its lock into independent memory, preserving row/index
state, deadlines, per-principal/global quota accounting and limits, epoch, ring
and generation binding. The copy never retains the live publisher or external
presence. A mismatched hub/store generation refuses. These are quiescent test
fixture copies, not live adoption or wire changes (LLP3000.017 §§3–4).
Durable generated initialization and histories then run normally in mixed apps.
Generated ephemeral populations and ephemeral mutation histories remain explicit
coverage gaps; their mutation programs are skipped rather than dispatched
through the durable mutation door. External presence still refuses a fixture
fork. Packed social must exercise actual native challenges, not a zero-work pass.

The unsupported ephemeral-program boundary applies in every dispatch phase,
including column/row paired observers and foreign-asset challenges, with a
central observer refusal as a backstop. No phase credits a skipped ephemeral
mutation as a reachable witness. Durable query controls still execute.
Column independence follows aliases into other written fields/tables as well
as guards. A creator copying an inserted private value into a related marker
constrains the alternate world; changing only the source value cannot turn
an always-public id list into a column disclosure. Alias-dependent operators
and opaque effects likewise stay conservatively uncovered.

Alias dependence is field-sensitive across compiler-owned object projections:
a copied row id does not couple an independently supplied private payload.
Unknown, defaulted or implicit upsert field sources remain uncovered. An
immutable-column alternate that must rebuild the SQL fixture declines when
the original hub has committed ephemeral state; it must not reset that state
and attribute the resulting public-presence difference to a protected column.
Actual typed disclosures and state-preserving forks remain available.

Roster discovery reuses the authority classifier's global principal/role index
relationship. A payload column merely named `role` does not bypass its authored
creator; drafts/final reports preserve creator constants and integrity checks.
The ephemeral exclusion also applies to operation-specific synthetic probes.
HTTP checks recognize existing route-specific authentication refusals: queries
and durable mutations use401 denied.E_AUTH; declared ephemeral mutations may
use200 failed/why.E_AUTH. Both require the auth family, no application-rule
marker and no successful data/result. Apply this equally to unsigned/forged
requests and actually retired credentials. Live wire semantics do not change.

The generated roster bootstrap exemption additionally requires an actual
viewer/role authority probe in a schema gate. A principal key alone does not
make a `role` or `kind` payload field an authority seat; such created tables
execute their authored creator. Program-only authority whose bootstrap cannot
be established within those creators is left explicitly unpopulated rather
than inventing payload states. Role actor proposals remain available.

Bootstrap proposals execute an ordinary insert in an isolated fixture fork,
including supplied-field ownership, sequence authority, defaults, recorded
fields, table gates and final constraints. A temporary private probe program
is removed after execution and never adopted or served. Rejected proposals
are uncovered; no system actor or trusted seeding bypass is used for admission.
## 14. SEC5: specification questions and refutations that land

Implementer: Codex, SEC5, 2026-10-21. The wave-20 bakeoff found zero
specification-shaped read invariants among 228 declarations. This amendment
changes analysis and presentation; it grants no runtime authority. DX5 owns
staging clocks, hidden lookup diagnostics, upgrades and frontend scaffolding.
The controlling proof and fixture-origin boundaries in §§3 and 13 remain.

### 14.1 Elicit the specification

The restatement advisory covers equivalent predicates and structural weaker
copies where effective admission implies intent in the existing three-valued
kernel and the policy atoms already occur in enforcement (including equivalent
viewer-keyed/named-key probes). Added independent policy alternatives do not
become restatements merely because they widen authority. Both
are advisory: an equivalent simple owner policy may be exactly right. Unknown
or exhausted implication work cannot establish a copy. Column comparisons use
the effective row AND column gate; update comparisons retain their image rules.
Owned-field bypasses and implicit effects are part of the judged write gate;
decline the advisory when that gate cannot be represented soundly.
Each message identifies the affected rule, admitted role branches and row
conditions as questions: should this role see these rows, in these states,
and these columns? It must not invent a product restriction. Questions describe
conditional admission, not proof that a principal or operation reaches it.

The generated draft is deterministic commented questions for each discovered
role and table/column, including ownership, relationship and lifecycle prompts.
It contains no proposed rule copies and is never compiled or accepted
automatically. The installed README/start packet teaches: answer from the
product requirement, write independent intent, run check and test, inspect
audit, repair enforcement while preserving that intent. Test all changed
snippets as written against a fresh packed package.

### 14.2 Advice without declared invariants

Notes remain non-failing, source-located and conditional; each names a rule or
operation, its admitted role/condition and a concrete repair. Analyze bound
structure, not a role name's presumed privilege. Retain conjunctions when
walking disjunctions: `(roster OR owner) AND owner` is not roster-wide.

* Broad-read advice includes an unrestricted viewer-keyed roster branch inside
  an OR, with the same structural criterion used for the whole-rule note.
* Role-wide advice includes a pure role branch on a table with a lifecycle enum
  and sensitive payload (principal, private field or media), or existing
  row-filtered private/media shapes. An extra row gate remains part of the
  reported condition. Recommend enforcing the intended states/relationship or
  separating the board projection; never assume which lifecycle states are safe.
* Relationship advice identifies an update/upsert existing-row writer that assigns viewer
  into an authority-bearing relationship after checking only the caller's role
  and an unclaimed/null value. Follow read-rule reference dependencies when the
  relationship grants access elsewhere. Owner self-edits and independently
  authorized delegation must not be mislabeled. Insert-only enrollment is outside
  this takeover note. "Only" means no independently verified approval/invitation
  condition in the effective gate or dominating requirements. Recommend trusted approval or
  independently verified invitation/relationship, rather than self-assertion.
* Caller-time advice traces time arguments and derived expressions into stored
  time fields used by deadline/lifetime comparisons. A dominating appropriate
  server-now bound can discharge the advice; a bound on a different argument,
  a one-sided bound in the wrong direction, or a non-dominating branch cannot.
  A bound dominates only when it holds on every control-flow path reaching the
  write. This narrow unbounded-time note is discharged by an upper or lower bound
  against server now on every path; it does not decide from names whether the
  specification requires past-only, future-only or a two-sided window. Opposite
  one-sided guards in disjunction do not form a shared bound. Comparisons must constrain the actual
  argument/expression being stored; job/schedule periodStart/periodEnd arguments
  are trusted scheduler boundaries, not caller-provided clocks. Recommend server now for event timestamps, explicit server-now bounds for
  scheduled times and guarded monotonic transitions for existing deadlines.

Do not infer a confirmed vulnerability from advice alone. Pin note deltas on
the 250 earlier schemas (wave 15: 100; waves 16, 18 and 19: 50 each), plus
wave 20's 50, making 300 frozen schemas. Record wave 21's currently available
sources separately as a changing supplementary set; inspect newly emitted
sites against their specifications. No invariant-free source may acquire a
security refusal. Intent contradictions already present in authored declarations
are reported separately from false refusals.

### 14.3 Concrete role refutations and coupled worlds

When direct read implication fails, bounded check targets the admitted role
branch with existing generated-fixture admission and authored observation paths.
Bootstrap proposals must satisfy an ordinary insert rule before insertion;
payload creators run their actual mutation guards. Final schema validation,
unique/reference constraints, raw policy evaluation and the actual authored
read/column projection must succeed. A forbidden disclosed value with policy
Denied establishes a GeneratedFixtureTrace refutation and fails check. Boolean
SAT assignments, Unknown, failed seeding, invented query payloads and unavailable
authored observations never establish such a failure. Keep the generated
initial-state assumption visible; do not call it an empty-state creation trace.
The work shares the existing bounded-check budget; exhaustion remains unproved.

Column paired worlds preserve every non-protected base value, including recorded
or captured values reconstructed from creator writes. Only the protected
column changes. Both worlds retain the same viewer, arguments, clock and
validated non-protected state; validate and repeat the actual observer in each.
Creator-coupled columns are independently perturbable only under §13.7's existing
reduction: copied/computed/constant writes and arguments reused by a guard or
another expression do not qualify merely because reseeding validates. If this
reduction cannot hold its premises, report the reason as unproved. Pairs retain
AuthoredFixtureTrace or GeneratedFixtureTrace from their actual initial fixture
and fail only on a repeated authored observation with policy Denied in both
worlds; synthetic/seed-only pairs remain supplemental. Every compact failure,
JSON witness and selection/count retains the origin and starting assumptions.
Presence/absence experiments with image/file columns fork one hydrated verified
asset ledger. Independent uploads with unequal identities cannot establish a
paired witness. Preserve one-home placement, typed media validation and final
reference constraints; do not weaken production checks to admit test fixtures.

### 14.4 Actions precede coverage details

Default check and audit lead with concrete failing repairs and ranked review
actions, followed by proved/refuted/unproved counts. Distinguish confirmed
refutations from advisory specification questions. Include source, role/rule,
one-line repair and the next executable command. Unproved remains explicit
but its repeated explanations, exclusions and full conditional exposure map
belong in verbose/all output and JSON. No unresolved item becomes proved or
disappears from structured output. Retain all failing sites and authority-
escalation advice even past the ordinary five-note cap. Every compact failing
line explicitly carries its fixture origin. Avoid a passing claim
beside a failing exit; no-notes/no-invariants never means zero security holes.

### 14.5 Qualification

Measure ten fresh builders on disposable wave-20 copies using only installed
docs and each product specification; save prompts, provenance, resulting
invariants and catches. On the seven confirmed-finding lanes compare no intent,
original intent and independently specification-shaped intent, preserving source
hashes and distinguishing flagged advice, replayed catches and uncovered work.
The seven wave-20 lanes are 001, 006, 025, 033, 036, 048 and 050, covering the
eight originally reported highs (three calibrated highs and five mediums) plus
006's medium derived-column disclosure. Earlier SEC3/SEC4 denominators describe
their historical qualification and do not replace this wave's denominator.
Zero false refusals is the acceptance bar: zero new security refusals for
invariant-free sources, and every newly refusing declared invariant must have a
replayed violation of independently confirmed intent. Pin before/after source
hashes, original-source contradictions and any corrections separately.

| Wave-20 fix | Clause | Acceptance witness |
|---|---|---|
| #1 specification intent | §14.1 | ten fresh builders; role/column questions; 050 intake and 006 diagnosis audience |
| #2 no-invariant advice | §14.2 | 048 roster OR; 001/033/036 lifecycle; 006 claimChild; 025 caller deadline |
| #3 refutations | §14.3 | 050 excluded intake fails check; 036 photo worlds expose forbidden sharpener read |
| #5 actionable output | §14.4 | compact check/audit leads with repair/review, preserves origin and full JSON |

Run full requested crate/client, differential, Lean, packed Linux, wasm, corpus,
reference, docs and repo-root checks. Every shell command is time-bounded;
dispose of owned scratch and large unneeded build outputs. Record actual
failures/limits in WORKSTREAM-REPORT.md. No version bump, push or publication.

### 14.6 Controlling inherited premises and review clarifications

This section changes only the explicitly named reductions and presentation.
Sections 3, 8.1, 13.5, 13.6 and 13.7 apply **in full**, including all their
creator-coupling, alias/object, guard-alias, implicit/default/upsert, ephemeral
hub, final-exists, unknown and provenance restrictions. Summaries in §14 do
not enumerate or replace those premises. A refutation requires policy Denied,
never Unknown, over the same committed read-back, viewer and clock as the
actual authored observer; evaluate using the engine's exact three-valued leaf
semantics before changing any authority dependency.

The lint-only normalization of named probe pairs and current-row self-id probes
does not enter proofs. A denied gate is not restatement evidence. Weaker-copy
advice requires each normalized policy atom to follow from the effective gate
and implication under the enriched gate; unfamiliar independent alternatives
remain quiet. Unrepresentable write bypasses decline the comparison.
The questionnaire supersedes the draft-alternative wording of §§10.3 and 13.5:
only stable sorted commented questions, rewritten only when content changes.

Broad-OR analysis judges complete disjuncts, retaining every conjunct. Universal
branches use `is_uniformly_readable`; roster branches must be viewer-keyed
membership probes. Unknown predicates never establish an admitted audience.
The new row-wide shape requires a mutable non-role enum, a competing field or
reference relationship and a globally role-gated branch which does not already
imply row-creation authority. Existing row-filtered private/media shapes retain
their guards. Enum values are a prompt to check lifecycle intent, not an inferred
product state machine. Protected-column alternatives are reviewed under their
actual intersected row/column gate; creation does not establish continuing
column readership. Advice names the conditional branch, not a proved reachability.

Authority-bearing relationship fields must pair viewer with a row key in a raw
read-rule grant probe. A self-claim requires a viewer assignment and an admitted
existing-row branch with only global caller probes and null/self relationship
tests; scoped invitation, resource-owner or independent approval evidence
declines that narrow note. Caller-time bounds use lower/upper direction bits:
AND unions bounds; OR retains only directions shared by both branches. Either
direction discharges this narrow absence-of-a-bound note; it does not certify
the specification's permitted window. Comparisons constrain the actual argument
with at most fixed numeric offsets against server now with fixed offsets;
arbitrary arithmetic or a different caller argument is not a server bound.
A mutation argument is never trusted merely because its name resembles a
scheduler clock. Job schedule boundaries are used only to identify stored
fields consumed by scheduled deadline checks.

Both paired worlds share **one Store-owned hydrated ledger fork**. Required
media without that coupled ledger remains unproved with a reason. Preserve
one-home placement, media verification, repeatability and final-exists validation.
Column-value fixture observers retain their actual fixture origin and may fail;
synthetic row-absence/deletion pairs stay synthetic and non-failing.

Check notes retain §13.5's tier/consequence/site/code/message ordering, five-note
cap with explicit remainder and all escalation notes. Audit applies the same
security-consequence order. Every failing line carries source, viewer/rule,
kebab-case fixture origin, repair and next command. Verdict totals count all
Refuted statuses and label the failing subset and supplemental observations
separately; unproved is the exact remaining status count, never proof by omission.

Qualification pins executable digest, source and fixture bytes, tarball identity
and installed-doc version. Fresh sessions receive only their disposable copy,
original specification and installed docs; save prompts and transcripts. Caught
means a failing authored/generated fixture trace with Denied on independently
confirmed intent. Advice is flagged, synthetic evidence is supplemental, and
unsupported cases are uncovered. Retain §13.6's distinction between a false
refusal and an original declared-policy contradiction (including omitted seats
or whole-row privacy over intentional public totals), preserving both original
and corrected outcomes.

The builder comparison uses the ten lanes' frozen original wave-20 builder
outputs with installed 0.4.35 as its historical baseline, and ten fresh sessions
on the candidate tarball as its after measurement, matched by specification.
Pin both installed-document/package identities and the starting declarations;
compare specification-shaped invariants, confirmed catches and review/repair
actions. This is an observational before/after comparison with different agents,
not a randomized causal A/B claim. The wave-20 baseline's zero specification-
shaped read invariants is independently classified from the original source.

### 14.7 SEC5 final review dispositions

All earlier sections of this RFC remain normative except where §14 explicitly
supersedes them. In particular §§10.1/10.4/10.5 paired worlds and pullbacks,
§12.5 observer census and §12.8 dependency closure are unchanged. No new
derived-observer reduction is claimed by this workstream.

Generated bootstrap admission and authored creator execution are implementation
premises under §13.7, not claimed initializer history. Witness `supporting_rows`
contains the committed initial population. `history` records actual subsequent
calls with viewer/arguments/results; transition credit requires its committed
role/relationship-changing call, never stale arranged starting rows. 050's
static catch is a GeneratedFixtureTrace observation under that initial-state
assumption. 036's lifecycle catch records its actually admitted successor calls
under the same generated assumption. Compact origin and qualification retain
this distinction; neither catch claims empty-state creation reachability.

Column witnesses pin `row`, `alternate`, `supporting_rows`, `alternate_rows`,
viewer/arguments and present/absent observation values. Their real media ids
must agree outside the protected difference. Ordering is: hydrate/verify uploads
once, fork that Store-owned ledger into the two worlds, seed and validate both,
revalidate final-exists, run each authored observer twice at identical clock and
arguments, compare repeatable observations. There is no independent upload per
world and no claimed durable ledger-fork receipt. Tests enforce those control
paths; unavailable media or unequal ids decline with a named coverage reason.
Production `row_image` normalization equates sparse optional/literal-default
representations before comparing non-protected fields; it adds no invented
field values or creator independence assumption.

The check budget is §13.5's shared **512 call/world reservations and 1500 ms**
safety deadline. Each generated world/history step reserves one; a paired
experiment reserves four observer calls, also subject to the existing 16384
column-call cap. Target direct role observers before generic history prefixes;
ordering is deterministic. Exhaustion retains exactly the existing work/time
budget coverage reason and never awards proof. Full tests prioritize admitted
lifecycle-changing successor mutations with at most 256 attempts, 16 frontier
worlds and depth three, recording their real calls. This changes search order,
not admission or proof assumptions. Qualification records attempted work and
any exhausted budget per lane; no deadline guarantees discovery under load.

For row-wide advice, mutable means an enum not declared immutable (not proof
that a particular writer changes it). Role-bearing enum columns are excluded
by the role-probe census. Relationship signal means an actual Field/Reference
term in a competing read alternative. The pure-role branch must contain only
global viewer/literal probes, and `kernel::implies(branch, ordinary insert gate)`
must not be proved true. Row-filtered branches retain every written conjunct.
The grouped protected-column note names its real intersected audience and asks
about each role. These are factual exposure questions, not inferred violations.

One-sided bounded caller times are deliberately outside the new **unbounded**
time note, preserving quiet advice for valid timestamps and scheduled events.
The note neither certifies a two-sided product window nor promises to detect
every time-rule mistake. The 025 unbounded flows are qualified explicitly.
Intentional self-claim pools may confirm that design; the repair says to confirm
self-claim intent and otherwise require independent approval/invitation.

Restatement's weaker-copy condition is whole implication plus policy-atom
occurrence in enforcement (or the named lint-only probe equivalences). Adding
an independent alternative excludes that weaker-copy note. These operations
never change proof results. Equivalent legitimate simple policies may still
receive a confirmation question, never a refusal.

The human templates are `FIX <source>: <target> via <operation> as <viewer>
(<fixture-origin>); tighten enforcement to the declared intent, then run
snapback4 test`, and `REVIEW <source> <code>: <conditional audience question>`
followed by its repair. Origin tokens are `authored-fixture-trace` and
`generated-fixture-trace`; synthetic evidence is explicitly non-failing. With
no invariants, the next action is to answer the generated draft from the spec,
declare intent and run test; zero declared intent never means security passed.

The frozen 300 survey runs original as-authored sources before and after.
Run an additional invariant-stripped pass to assert zero security refusals;
report original-declaration contradictions separately. The seven-finding-lane
matrix runs stripped, original and specification-shaped intent. Numeric
measurement completeness is ten fresh builder sessions on ten matched specs,
with all resulting declarations, independent classifications and catches
reported. The improvement target is specification-shaped read intent above
the historical baseline of zero, not an invented universal-security threshold.

### 14.8 Audit is a map; test runs the full finite campaign

The installed-doc builder experiment exposed an existing performance trap:
`audit --json` ran full generated campaigns before returning any permission map.
This explicitly supersedes §13.3's full-campaign default for audit. Audit uses
the same shared 512-reservation/1500ms bounded evidence path as check, including
matching recorded full-test refutations and the exact selected fixture rules.
Its map, invariant statuses and missing-intent questions are always available;
unvisited or exhausted paths remain unproved with their reasons in JSON/all.
Neither `audit --all` nor verbosity expands the search budget; they expose all
stored report detail. `test` remains the full finite campaign verb and records
its completed evidence using the existing identity/source/fixture fingerprint.

Test prints that full finite campaigns are running before starting them. It
keeps every actual failing observation and a bounded first five coverage reasons
in ordinary output, with an explicit remainder count; `test --all` or verbose
shows every reason. This is presentation and scheduling of analysis, never a
proof result, a runtime authority change, or a new passing-evidence cache.

### 14.9 Corpus precision refinements

The before/after survey identified legitimate global staff audiences and category
enums. These refinements narrow the **new advisory** eligibility in §§14.2/14.6/
14.7; they do not change proof, runtime rules, or the existing top-level broad
and row-filtered media notes.

An added OR-roster note requires a heterogeneous role-bearing roster, a competing
viewer/resource relationship and an explicit column-privacy boundary. This
retains 048's family/staff mix without questioning every owner-or-single-admin
table. New pure role-wide row advice requires an actual authored literal enum
update/upsert, a comparison of the same bound row field, and media or explicit
column privacy on that resource or a referencing child. Group roles and their
complete conditions once per table; preserve every admitted alternative in the
question. Grouped protected-column questions retain the real intersected gate
and ask about the explicitly protected columns; creator authority does not
prove continuing private-column readership. Cases outside these detectors are
uncovered by this advice, not safe by inference.

The unbounded-time note propagates lower/upper server-now bounds through
dominating affine comparisons between time arguments, including fixed offsets
and optional argument/server-now fallbacks. Use a finite fixed point: an
unanchored cycle cannot invent a clock bound. Conjunction unions directions;
disjunction retains shared directions. Service-seat-only operations are outside
ordinary caller-clock advice only when dominating authority establishes that
exact seat restriction; mixed seat/ordinary branches remain eligible. Jobs stay
outside caller-argument advice. This is an advisory discharge, never a theorem
of the specification's allowed time window. Tests retain unbounded 025 inputs,
transitive bounded endpoints, no-anchor cycles and mixed-authority negatives.

The installed questionnaire asks about own and unrelated rows separately for
users without a roster role. Compare positive grants with product workflows
before retaining access to another person's private data. One table-read
invariant may cover fields sharing one intended audience; use column invariants
for distinct privacy or independent protection, rather than mechanically
writing one declaration for every question. These post-measurement clarifications
address the observed duplicate-declaration cost; the first ten builders' exact
input tarball and resulting limitations remain recorded without relabeling them.

SEC5 scoped amendments §§14.1–14.9: Muse and fresh Codex READY;
[verbatim review provenance](./reviews/3000.036.004-sec5-review-record.md).

## 15. SEC6: write boundaries, returned privacy and deployment review

Implementer: Codex, 2026-10-21, commissioned by wave-21 SEC6. EVOL owns
adoption, rollback fidelity, migration expressiveness and release preview narration.
Section 14 is reserved for SEC5, integrating concurrently on main.
This amendment adds analysis and client validation; runtime authorization stays
with the existing rules. No language spelling or authority exemption is added.

### 15.1 Every executed write

0.4.40's precision clarification is [LLP 3000.036.004.000 §9](./3000.036.004.000-structural-write-authority.rfc.md#9-integration-24-wave-26-precision): authored column write intent exempts null/unapproved-false initialization only on insert, while changed edits retain both-image authority. It does not imply mandatory creation presence. The operator's exact own provision result has an explicit narrow observation-scope exception; ordinary queries and other returned images remain checked. Structural `write <-` still counts non-null defaults. The earlier SEC6 amendment below retains its history; the linked clarification governs these boundaries.

Column `write only` intent covers insert, insert-if-absent's inserted arm,
update, and both upsert arms. `update only` remains intentionally edit-only;
creation may legitimately have different authority. Missing intent advice asks
about an authored insert/upsert path whose admission does not establish its
update restriction. It is a conditional review question, never a refusal or an
inferred product specification. Operation requirements and literal/default
payloads narrow the advice where the implemented reduction establishes them.

Finite history challenges must observe actual executed write boundaries, not
infer them from a net before/after image. Opt-in, host-only fixture instrumentation
judges supported independent policy predicates immediately before each physical
insert/update/delete against that transaction's current snapshot. Updates judge
old and next images; column updates (whether targeted by `write only` or `update only`) require an actual column change. No-op and
if-absent collision arms are not inserted writes. The trace includes intermediate
writes subsequently overwritten, loops, and delete/reinsert. Only a committed
ordinary authored operation contributes failure-eligible evidence; a rolled-back
operation contributes no write violation. Seed/synthetic origins retain their
non-failing classification. Unknown/error judgments are coverage gaps.
Instrumentation has a separate reader and meter, cannot alter product admission,
and is unavailable on the wire and the web device. No finite trace proves intent.
The hook is Context::with_fixture_trace, absent by default and not deserialized.
The physical insert/replace/delete primitives invoke it after authorization and
validation, before their store write. Collision/no-op branches never reach it;
upsert arm identity follows the primitive actually executed, not write_tables.
It creates a fresh Runner over the same provisional transaction with a separate
unbounded meter, authorization budget, caches and observation state. It does not
change the application Runner or serialize anything into its outcome. A Created
predicate is unknown until created-fact instrumentation is supported. The caller
discards the entire trace unless mutate returns Sent. Policy errors never alter
the product result. Implicit effects without an instrumented boundary remain
explicitly uncovered. Such missing hooks do not invalidate neighboring instantaneous
judgments, but every cross-boundary stability claim inherits §12.8's transitive
implicit-target and read-authority-dependency closure. No new kernel axiom.

### 15.2 Mutation results are observations

Column privacy includes implicit write results, explicit return objects and
authored refusals. A query projection is only one observer's shape. Located
no-invariant advice identifies columns omitted from an authored query projection
or guarded by a column rule that another authored result/refusal may observe.
It names the operation and column and asks for independent spec intent; it does
not claim that every returned private column violates the intended audience.

Actual compiler-typed mutation row payloads are checked against column read
intent as queries are. This reduction requires a stable necessary policy across
the mutation and a surviving committed image; ambiguous intermediate/changed
policy (using §12.8 and stability::implicit/dependencies closures), derived values or refusal dependencies require existing paired-world
replay or remain uncovered. Equal scalar values alone are not source provenance.
Hidden/omitted payloads and safe projections must not create a failure.
For a typed returned row, all row-local policy operands (including reference
source columns) must be present and equal to the judged committed image. A
saved readable row returned after its owner changes is uncovered, not refuted
against the new owner. External rule/read-authority dependencies must remain
unchanged through the transitive closure. The captured server clock is injected
into instrumented judgment images as in existing fixture_policy replay.
Declared read intent constrains actual typed output even for insertion echoes;
a creator's supplied or copied values are not an implicit privacy exemption.
Three prior query-coupling controls returned their protected field from a writer;
retain their query intent but project writer ids, and separately assert the old
full-row returns now refute. No weakening of the independent invariant. The reviewed direct runtime reductions are WriteTarget's final ordinary get
followed by columns::write_result, and insertion's ProjectFields builtin using
project_row_if_readable with column rules. Explicit return expressions consume
already projected authored values. Refusal and derived paths are not claimed to
be universally reduced by those two cases: paired observers and explicit blockers
remain. Regress both server insertion and server update result redaction. This
work never treats a query projection as an authorization rule.

The insert-intent and result-exposure review notes remain visible in short `check` output outside the ordinary note cap, following the existing escalation precedent. Nullable creation arguments retain null-first population coverage; direct optional column-write challenges interleave non-null alternatives within the existing finite cap. Missing commit-end images from expiry report coverage only and never stand in for an executed delete.

### 15.3 Rule-changing deployment review

Both deploy and deploy --check lead with outstanding security review items when
bound row/column/owner rules change against the selected live release (including
added/deleted tables). Print every refuted/unproved declaration and each security
note without an ordinary note cap, with source, reason, repair/next command.
Display the last recorded test time, age, completion, reachable violations and
coverage gaps; identify stale source/fixture/CLI evidence explicitly. No record
means never tested, not passed. Matching tests remain finite evidence, not proof.
Timestamp metadata is outside the input fingerprint. Record latest-run time and
outcome separately from last-completed-campaign time; an incomplete run may update
the former but never refresh the latter. Persist optional timestamps with
existing input-bound records;
legacy records say age unknown rather than inventing a date. A later incomplete
run cannot erase earlier established refutations on the same inputs.

Ordinary deploy remains advisory. Explicit --strict refuses rule-changing
previews and activation on failure-eligible reachable refutations (fresh bounded
analysis or matching persisted evidence), before any activation. Analysis, persisted evidence and narration resolve the
same saved-source companion fixtures once (an explicit --fixture overrides). Unproved items,
notes, stale evidence and synthetic-only refutations are not strict refusals.
Matching persisted eligible failures suffice even when bounded resampling is
skipped; skipped work is never described as fresh exploration. Refutations lead
the text; unproved declarations are one line per target and notes follow, avoiding
large per-obligation expansions. Keep all items visible as the author requested.
No stricter meaning is attached to passing finite tests. JSON retains the review
text and stderr prominently prints it; no preview/private snapshot mutates live
release data. Review follows the selected saved source and fixtures.

### 15.4 Supplied client sessions

DX6 amends the online legacy-bearer case in [LLP 3000.000.000.001 §11](./3000.000.000.001-staging-with-real-users.rfc.md#11-wave-22-loop-repairs-dx6); complete/local validation remains as below.

Online and local constructors validate a supplied session before any transport,
device/storage work or timer. Require a non-array object with nonempty principal,
kind and token strings, a positive finite safe-integer expiresAt in milliseconds,
and valid optional scope/capExpiresAt fields. Legacy scope-less sessions remain
accepted. Expiry relative to a possibly skewed local clock is server authority;
shape validation must not reject a structurally valid past timestamp. Malformed
input throws E_SESSION with the offending field and a repair: pass the complete
Session returned by guest/login/signup or service minting, or use the existing
online token option for a bare bearer. Never include credentials in the error.
capExpiresAt without scope remains accepted legacy metadata; neither grants read authority.
Stored-session validation retains its existing auth restore path. Snapshot validated primitive fields so caller mutation cannot manufacture a NaN
timer later. Existing intentional full-session refresh and read/action expiry
semantics remain unchanged; shape validation grants no authority.

### 15.5 Qualification

Regress insert/upsert/if-absent and multi-write/rollback/role-change boundaries,
optional non-null samples, mutation payload/privacy safe controls, strict deploy
and evidence-age/staleness, malformed sessions before side effects and valid
legacy/read/action sessions. Re-measure copies of seven wave-21 lanes, both
without invariants (advice) and with spec intent plus test (replayed catches).
The seven lanes are 001,006,023,033,036,040,048, plus 048's carried-over
riders.read finding. Freeze copied .q bytes and their SHA256 manifest under
reports/20261021-sec6 before experiments; raw apps remain read-only. Report a
per-finding flagged/caught/partial/not-caught table, never conflating advice and
replay. Optional arguments must include null and a non-null typed value, with a
non-null riderGoals insert witness. The false-refusal corpus is the user-requested
waves 15,16,18–21; wave17 can be supplementary but is not silently part of that bar.
Record new advice counts and specification-safe controls separately from refusals.
Measure false refusals on waves 15,16,18–21; do not label conditional advice as
a caught exploit. Packed guide qualification executes changed examples as written.


SEC6 qualification recorded2026-10-21 at product-code snapshot4578e98bed:
round6 Muse and fresh Codex both READY; all seven reported-high lane experiments
and the separate048 roster intent have actual generated-fixture witnesses.
Published0.4.35 baseline and candidate350-app checks (as written and invariant
stripped) have zero new refusals. The full seven-crate Rust inventory passes7234
tests,1159 executions plus doctests; client repairs/models and designated Mac
checks pass. Fresh packed consumer qualification passes Ubuntu24.04 and Alpine,
including the installed guide and SEC6 security examples. Consumer-only evidence
is separate from publication qualification. Detailed scope, original failed
attempts and hashes: [SEC6 report](../WORKSTREAM-REPORT.md) and
[evidence](../snapback4/reports/20261021-sec6/README.md). These finite results do
not assert universal security or add a kernel axiom.

## 16. SEC7: advice that reaches the builder

Implementer: Codex for Charlie Cheever, 2026-10-22. Wave-22 commission;
DX6 owns session/upgrade/clock and frontend/proof-kit fixes. No version bump.
This section supersedes §15.2's special display cap for insert/result notes and
§12.3's advisory-only boundary only for the two explicit gates below.
It also revises §§13.5/14.2/14.5/14.6: disposition-clearable review gates are
a new exit-1 category alongside replayed intent violations, including without
invariants. Their zero-new-refusal bars continue to bind failures no disposition
can clear (compilation failures and reachable refutations). Gate counts and
legitimate review costs are reported separately, never called replayed exploits. Runtime
authority and the proved/refuted/unproved contract remain unchanged.

### 16.1 Presentation and conditional questions

A shared severity ordering puts gated notes and likely-hole questions before
coverage, restates and result inventories. Default check retains every priority
0–2 likely-hole question and the first five ordinary code classes in severity
order, keeping each selected class's distinct sites/audiences; SEC6 insert/result
notes never increase that budget. Result exposure notes group by source table, retaining every operation,
column, observer kind and source site in JSON/full text. Audit uses the same
severity and numeric file/line/column ordering, never lexicographic site strings.
Insert-write advice requires an explicitly supplied nonliteral expression whose
flow uses a mutation argument; omitted defaults and literal-only assignments are
quiet. These are precision changes to advice, never creation-policy inference.

New advisory classes ask about: reason text without a positive non-whitespace
minimum; optional money supplied to creation; constant true payment/approval
flags (a naming heuristic, explicitly a question); competing generic enum setters
versus stricter literal transitions; mutable creation checks omitted by a later
transition or derived creation; inconsistent server-now duration bounds on the
same bound column across operations; and global-role operations recording viewer
on an unrelated row. Restating questions and the draft ask whether each role may
write any row, independently of the read questionnaire. The relationship recipe
models the owner of this wall explicitly. These shapes cannot infer the spec.

### 16.2 Narrow gates and individual dispositions

N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE: an optional image/file mutation argument flows to an
insert/upsert of a media column, including a conditional child insertion. A
non-null guard around just the child does not establish required parent evidence.
An unconditional dominating require of non-null before any creating write can
exclude the note; aliases/helpers are resolved by the existing authority walker.
Optionality may be intentional: the gate requires review, never asserts an exploit.

N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT: a read branch trusts a retained grant; an authored grant
writer requires a literal role on the principal argument stored as that grant's
reader principal; that role is mutable through authored operations; and the read
branch does not imply the same current-reader role. Require a positive recipient
role atom in every admitting writer branch; do not mistake the assigner's role
for the recipient's. The gate excludes ordinary owner branches, literal service
seats and mere caller-role requirements. Revoking derived grants is a repair;
static inability to prove full revocation remains reviewable, not an automatic
safety theorem. General N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE stays advisory.

Unreviewed gates make check exit 1 with structured note IDs, without marking an
invariant refuted. The existing bulk --ack-notes cannot clear a security gate.
Use check --ack-security <id> --reason '<spec rationale>' for one current note.
Store versioned dispositions in snapback/security-reviews.json with ID, reason,
source digest and analyzer revision. IDs hash the complete diagnostic; the source
digest hashes all compiled source filenames and bytes in sorted order. Every
source edit, changed diagnostic or analyzer revision invalidates the disposition.
Blank reasons, unknown IDs, malformed files and incompatible flag combinations
refuse. Writes use temp+rename and verify compiled sources still match disk;
failed compilation/refuted intent must never record acknowledgments. Dispositions
are review records, never authority or proof, remain visible in JSON/audit and
can be checked into source control. Broad advice may also be individually reviewed.

### 16.3 Directed finite histories

Keep the 512 breadth/pair budget. Add a separate bounded directed frontier before
breadth exploration for schema enum transitions and mutable dependencies of
independent intent/creator checks. Generate successors from the current world,
so references returned/created at one step are available later. Track semantic
state signatures over status/role/boolean/relationship fields, rather than minted
IDs alone; prioritize unseen lifecycle signatures and schema-relevant mutations.
Bound depth to eight authored mutations, attempts to 2048 and retained frontier
to 16 worlds; share check's existing work/deadline cap and report every cut.
Actual mutation admission, executed-write judgments and ordinary observers remain
the only failure oracle. Synthetic witnesses remain REVIEW and never failures.
Validated generated initial fixtures retain their explicit provenance. No seed
state is relabeled application reachable, and finite exhaustion remains unproved.

### 16.4 Qualification

Regression tests cover positive and negative shapes, helper/alias guards,
per-note/source/analyzer invalidation, failed-check non-writing, severity and
numeric ordering, and lifecycle/suspension histories beyond pairs. Measure every
wave-22 schema's default visibility and waves 15,16,18–22's gate counts separately
from false refusals. Optional evidence can legitimately fail pending a disposition;
report that cost explicitly rather than claim every gate is a confirmed hole.
Re-run copies of the eight high lanes without intent, with authored intent, and
with one independent spec-worded invariant. Advice is flagged; only a replayed
violation is caught. Packed qualification executes every changed guide snippet.

### 16.5 Review clarification: exact detector and command boundaries

These details resolve design review M1–M6. The implementation is the bounded
shape detector, not an inference that a schema satisfies its product specification.

Priority codes are fixed by security::note_priority: gates at 0; privilege and
self-claimed relationship at 1; grant-outlives, optional evidence, role-wide read,
broad read, caller time, sweep deadline and the seven new question codes at 2;
ordinary notes at 4; insert/result inventory at 5; restates at 6; intent coverage
at 7. The short list keeps every action at 0–2 plus the first five ordinary
code classes, preserving all sites/audiences within those classes. Repeated sites
of one ordinary code do not consume independent classes; insert/result codes
never enlarge the budget. This avoids hiding a second scan audience or a distinct
maintain diagnostic behind repeated bootstrap notes. Concrete
reachable refutations still print all failing sites, origins and repair commands
outside this list. A remainder count points to verbose/JSON. JSON retains every
note. Restates and coverage can appear only after the action classes fit.

Creation reason: an argument flows into an inserted/upserted column with `reason`
in its case-insensitive name and its text argument minimum is absent or zero.
Optional money: the optional argument flows into an optional Money column, or an
Int/IntRange/Decimal column whose lowercased name contains price, charge, fee or
amount. Constant approval: true literal assigned to a creating column containing
paid, approv or payment. All are name-based specification questions; no claim
covers other spellings. Required positive-minimum reasons and required money
arguments suppress their respective advice. One note per operation/column.

Transition bypass: same-table enum update from an enum argument can choose a
literal also written by another mutation, and its necessary effective admission
does not imply that other writer's bound requirements plus table gate. Resolve
row-local read operands to the target table's focal row for this advisory query.
A dominating contradictory literal argument guard excludes that target. Generic
setter/table/target/stricter-op duplicates collapse by exact diagnostic message.
A stricter-gate failure alone is advisory, never an inferred approval violation.
Stale creation: a resolved creating-write context reads a non-role dependency
that another mutation updates/upserts/deletes, and a different later mutation
updates the created table or writes a referencing child without reading that
dependency in its dominating context. Group by operation, created table and
dependency. Mere dependency reads do not prove eligibility; this heuristic's
false negatives and speculative questions are not gated.
Duration: compare the same resolved table/column against now +/- literal duration
with the same inequality direction in two operation contexts. Different offsets
produce one question listing all windows/operations. Other affine expressions,
custom wrappers and complex derived windows remain uncovered by this advice.
Role-wide write: a mutation updates a non-immutable Principal to viewer, has a
bound global role requirement, and lacks a dominating viewer-to-row-member
relationship comparison. It asks about this resource rather than declaring an
unauthorized write. The draft is bounded by discovered schema roles × tables;
it asks every pair about write authority, including pairs with authored intent.

Optional upload: the existing lexical authority walker resolves aliases and
helper arguments to the original mutation argument. Non-null suppression accepts
only a direct positive `argument != null` conjunct in an unconditional Require
before the first creating-write context. `$advice` branch guards cannot suppress;
late child guards, OR, unknown and unsupported expressions cannot suppress.
Known flows into asset fields are detected; arbitrary opaque/derived flows are
uncovered, never assumed safe. Both upsert arms retain the creation review question.
Retained role: use existing lints::mutable_role and privilege role-column/probe
resolution. Only direct positive Exists conjuncts with a literal recipient role
qualify, never an assigner/viewer-only test or negation. A branch suppresses only
when kernel::implies(branch, recipient-role-with-viewer) is Ok(true); false,
unknown and cap exhaustion retain the question. Dynamic role requirements stay
in general grant advice and are outside the narrow gate. Every noted branch
contains a positive grant probe binding that grant's principal column to viewer.
Owner equalities without such probes are excluded structurally. Role mutation
plus transactional grant revocation still needs review; it is not statically
proved safe or automatically refused as an exploit.

Disposition format is version 1: entries maps full diagnostic SHA256 IDs to
{sources, analyzer, reason}. JSON uses the existing Diagnostic serialization and
the sorted Vec<(filename, UTF-8 source bytes)> serialization used by Project.sources;
source names are schema-directory-relative (`name.q`). A line shift changes the
ID; any source edit changes all dispositions. This intentionally conservative
invalidation avoids unmodeled cross-table influence; optimization is deferred.
Analyzer is the explicit security_reviews::ANALYZER revision, bumped whenever
security detector meaning or disposition identity changes. A release alone does
not invalidate unchanged analysis. A reason must contain non-whitespace text.
No unrelated refuted invariant can be bypassed: any reachable refutation prevents
all acknowledgement writes and check still exits 1. Compilation errors likewise
write no security review. Read-only inspection remains available. Bulk --ack-notes
never addresses these records. --ack-security requires --reason and refuses
combination with --ack-notes, --fix, --store, --release, --emit-lean or --audit;
--json, --verbose, --cost and current-source fixture/residency inspection may
combine. Broad-advice dispositions change display, never exit status. Audit exits
on refuted intent as before and includes all notes/dispositions; new gates fail
check only. Deploy/preview/--strict keep §15.3; we recommend running check in acceptance before
deploy. Saved-source portability is not claimed for dispositions.

Directed exploration is primarily a test campaign: check retains the common
512-work/1500ms cap and may cut before it. No promise grants both directed and
breadth paths their full caps under check. Test retains its 512 singles/pairs
in addition to the directed bound. Successors fork the current complete fixture
through Engine::fork_fixture with its asset ledger; they use ordinary mutate,
NOW and existing executed/observer judgments. The signature keeps enum/bool/
principal/reference fields (with multiplicity), discarding other payload only as
a pruning heuristic. Such mergers and every frontier/argument/successor/depth cut
are explicitly uncovered. Retain up to four priority worlds (below the 16 maximum),
prefer changed enum states and true booleans, at most two new worlds per op/world,
four argument tuples, eight mutations and 2048 actual attempts. These finite
heuristics neither prove reachability nor clear an unproved verdict. Synthetic
matrix probes stay REVIEW; ordinary admitted histories from validated generated
initial fixtures retain GeneratedFixtureTrace and failure eligibility (§14).

Wave-22 high lanes are 004,011,021,026,030,037,040,044. Frozen source copies and
independent spec variants are in tests/security/wave22; the measurement manifest
records SHA256s. 037 remains the specification judgment case. With no invariants,
expect corresponding new/advisory notes on the seven nonjudgment lanes. Spec tests
must catch 026's terminal pickup and 030's suspend-before-approval history, alongside
existing 004/021/037 catches; 040/044 always/absent-money limits stay explicit.
Gate calibration targets: 021 for retained role; 003,018,027,040 for optional
uploads on wave22. 027 may be legitimate optionality: gate cost is review pending,
not a confirmed hole. Across waves15,16,18–22 report every project that fails a gate,
all compilation/refutation refusals separately, and every legitimate gate needing
a disposition. Zero false *unacknowledgeable* refusals is the acceptance criterion;
never relabel legitimate review gates zero false positives. Changed executable
guide material: wall-ownership Quarry in rule-recipes; existing cheatsheet,
security and upload-placement snippets are rerun from the fresh packed client.

### 16.6 Second-review resolutions

The two new gates are check-CI-only review obligations. Deploy still lists all
security notes in §15.3 review; its --strict boundary remains replayed refutations.
Running check in deployment acceptance is recommended, not an enforced fresh-check
receipt protocol. No saved-source deployment claim is made for these dispositions.
Acknowledged gates plus no other errors/refutations give exit 0. Default output
replaces each acknowledged note with its ID/code/reason review record; verbose,
JSON and audit retain the full note. Broad dispositions have this same display
effect with no exit-code effect. Stale entries remain in the file for review
history but do not acknowledge any current note. `check --json`, `check --verbose`
and audit inspect current notes without writing a disposition; malformed files
refuse with repair text. There is no wildcard or bulk security acknowledgment.

IDs use deterministic recursively key-sorted JSON diagnostic values; source digest
uses sorted source pairs. Tests require same-input/same-ID stability on repeated
checks in addition to invalidation controls. The analyzer revision is a reviewed
constant; changes to detector semantics or fingerprint serialization must bump it
and their tests must reject a previous revision. It is not a runtime security
boundary or promise that arbitrary modified binaries honor review semantics.

Transition advice emits only on definite Ok(false) implication, suppressing unknown
or capped comparisons as uncovered by that heuristic. Stale-creation, reason,
money, constant-approval and duration advice use finite structural matches only:
unknown/unsupported syntax does not match and is not inferred safe. Their documented
naming/type/binding fragment is their entire coverage, without a proof claim.
General retained-grant advice and the narrow gate may co-fire intentionally:
count gate projects by gated code and project, not total note count. Neither
advisory repeat creates another required disposition. This preserves existing
conditional-lifetime explanations while identifying the precise required review.

Successor generation, schema/program/actor ordering, argument tuples and stable
priority ties are deterministic for fixed inputs. Time/work cuts remain explicit.
Root generated populations are visited before derived creators mutate their
status/eligibility. This is a validated generated initial fixture, with the same
explicit GeneratedFixtureTrace origin; no root rows are invented terminal states.
The additional pre-transition pass is full-test-only, shares the campaign's
observer ceilings, and never runs recursively or increases check's budget.

SEC7 design review provenance: [verbatim reviews](./reviews/3000.036.004-sec7-review-record.md).

Implementation-review clarifications: upload suppression intersects the positive
non-null requirements of every creating-write context, rather than borrowing one
branch's guards. Recipient probes distinguish the original viewer from the stored
recipient, verify the roster principal column and retain only its literal role
requirement for current-role comparison. Result identity adds to existing source
lineage; it never replaces copied protected-column provenance. Role-wide-write
suppression requires a structural positive viewer-to-target relationship equality.
Directed search includes mutable role/boolean/relationship dependencies, uses two
prioritized actors per tuple (others remain in breadth), and immediately drops
displaced beam worlds. The retained-world high-water mark and shared directed
attempt counter are reported. Disposition writers serialize read-modify-write
through a kernel file lock in ignored generated output, with automatic release
on exit. These are precision/resource corrections within the reviewed contract.

Corpus calibration additionally narrows the retained-role gate to reader probes
whose declared key binds the stored recipient principal to viewer. A parent row
looked up by resource id with an additional owner/customer/parent equality is an
ownership relationship, not this assignment-key gate. The old declared-intent
principal-branch advisory is removed for the same reason; broad retained-grant
advice remains conditional. On wave22 this removes the owner-row questions from
001/009/041 while retaining 021's driver assignment. Directed argument selection
varies bool/enum decision arguments independently on resource identity tuples;
noise text cannot correlate a resource id with just the false branch. Successor
selection keeps distinct priority resource states, not the first two audit-actor
variants. Recipient bindings include implicit upsert-key columns, and effective
column read branches are checked with their row and owner gates. Directed
argument grouping and role-class actor deduplication each record uncovered
alternatives even when their subsequent numeric caps are not reached. The
empty-world directed pass defers population to ordinary generated
initialization/breadth. All omitted populations and variants remain uncovered.

### 16.7 Reviewed optionality in the stock social template

The existing social template intentionally permits text messages without photos.
Ship its single pre-reviewed source/analyzer-bound optional-photo disposition as
ordinary template data, with that explicit reason. This uses the same per-note
file and invalidation as user reviews, never a detector exemption or automatic
acknowledgment of arbitrary notes. The record names only that exact diagnostic
and schema; template/analyzer changes invalidate it and fail starter qualification
until reviewed again. Init beside an existing authored schema does not install
this stock-source disposition. The template README explains the decision. Packed
starter qualification must run init/check unchanged and verify the record becomes
stale after a source edit. No social API or runtime authority changes.

### 16.8 Typed upsert key binding

An unrelated unique index of the same arity must not erase a retained-recipient
grant question. The compiler already resolves positional keys by their types;
the security detector must consume that selected write-target index, not repeat
an arity-only approximation. Cache compiled write-target read-site indexes by
canonical source site and table once per source analysis. When they identify one
index, align the source write's named/positional key expressions with that bound
index's columns and merge the implicit stored fields with its explicit payload.
Keep the existing source pin resolver as a fallback for unambiguous source forms.
No planner/runtime rule or authorization changes: this repairs advice/gate recall
for the already documented shape. A two-unique-index recipient-key regression
and a reader that rechecks the role pin the gate and its suppression. Re-measure
the corpus for any newly recognized gates and report their individual review cost.

### 16.9 Day-two precision and destructive evidence questions

Implementer: Codex for Charlie Cheever, 2026-10-22; commissioned by the wave-23
day-two addendum. This supersedes the upload shape and stock-template disposition
in §§16.2/16.5/16.7 and narrows stale-creation advice. It changes analysis and
finite witness selection, never runtime admission or application authority.

**Optional uploads.** Optional arguments and optional columns are a valid model,
including deliberate v2 loosening and exceptions such as a lockout without proof.
Do not gate them merely because their names suggest evidence. Gate an optional
image/file argument written directly to an optional creating media column only
when a positive conjunct of an authored `table always` invariant requires that
exact column to be non-null. Flatten conjunctions and recognize
`field != null` and the exactly equivalent `not (field = null)` in either operand
order. Other negation, an OR, an unrelated column, comment, product document or unknown predicate is not
a mandatory-media declaration. No natural-language specification parsing, deploy
diff exemption, lane exemption or new intent language is introduced. Required
child media with optional arguments does not, by itself, require a child row.
A child-local non-null branch is valid optional attachment creation, including
040's return photo, and cannot fail check on that basis. Gate missing parent
evidence only when an independent parent `always exists child[...]` conjunct
pins the exact parent-reference relationship and the operation creates that parent.
Such a child-only guard cannot satisfy the explicit parent-level obligation.
Match the exact created parent reference and every additional literal child-probe
filter (e.g. checkout vs return). Unknown filters do not establish this gate.
An unconditional ordinary sibling insertion satisfying that same probe discharges
the obligation; an unrelated optional attachment must not be gated for it.
Conditional or if-absent sibling insertions cannot establish that discharge.
Without that obligation, parent-plus-conditional-child creation may ask the existing
advisory optional-evidence question, but never imposes an acknowledgment.
 Existing dominating non-null
guards across every creating context still suppress the question. Required scalar media (or a required nonempty list of required media)
with required arguments needs no optional-upload review. A required list allowing
zero elements is not mandatory evidence. Existing generic optional
evidence advice remains advisory. Bump the analyzer revision; remove the stock
social disposition and its special injection/custody code, because intended
text-only messages now check without any acknowledgment, including after edits.
Keep individual source-bound reviews for the two genuine gated shapes.

**Stale eligibility.** Match a creator's dominating condition on a non-role,
non-immutable column that another authored mutation actually changes (or an
existence condition on a deletable dependency). A mere read of an immutable owner
or an unrelated write to that table is not mutable eligibility. A later operation
qualifies only when it writes an approval-like literal enum state (`approved`,
`accepted`, `authorized`, `granted`, `issued`, `active`, `confirmed`, `paid`,
`signed`) or a true approval/authorization flag, and updates the created table
or creates a directly referencing child. An arbitrary edit, inspection, rejection,
cleanup or status-copy is outside this name-based advisory fragment. Rechecking
the relevant eligibility column (or named existence dependency) suppresses that
path; merely reading an immutable owner or a different column cannot suppress it. Group all creator and later-write
sites into one question per created table and mutable dependency; use the
lexicographically least (filename, numeric line, numeric column) decision site,
with sorted creator/decision names and sites plus the eligibility columns. This is a question, not an assertion that
every such decision must repeat the check; unsupported forms stay uncovered.

**Create/edit asymmetry.** Add `N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY`, ranked with likely-hole
questions (priority 2), and `N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY` at priority 1
for a write-only private payload. Compare
argument-supplied creation fields with actual authored updates to that field.
Generic table-wide differences remain ordinary insert inventory; the new classes
require a recovered column-specific conditional guard. Besides stable global
requirements and table update gates, recover a conditional
column guard of the form `if newValue != target.column: require authority`
(or the equivalent equality/else form). Resolve lexical aliases and helper
arguments. Match the exact table, selected target keys, column and assigned
expression; a guard for a different row/field cannot become this column's edit
guard. Only guaranteed leading requirements of the changed branch count. Mixed
conditions, unknown effects and guards separated by intervening writes do not
establish this match. This extraction is isolated to advice; it is not a proof
rule or a new runtime check. Emit only a definite failed implication from creation
admission to the stable edit guard; unknown/capped comparisons do not establish
