     1	//! Concrete question evidence; this never changes gate or invariant verdicts.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#19-wave-28-output-and-witness-decisions-dx11 — inferred witnesses never become authored refutations.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use snapback4_lang::{Diagnostic,security::challenges::{self,Counterexample}};
     5	use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
     6	fn hash(bytes:&[u8])->String {use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};format!("{:x}",Sha256::digest(bytes))}
     7	// Generated values only: retain typed scalars, redact opaque payloads.
     8	fn safe(value:&snapback4_core::Value,kind:Option<&ColumnType>)->serde_json::Value {
     9	    use snapback4_core::Value;
    10	    match (value,kind.map(ColumnType::required)) {
    11	        (_,Some(ColumnType::Text{..}|ColumnType::Json{..}|ColumnType::Bytes{..}|ColumnType::Image{..}|ColumnType::File{..}|ColumnType::Video{..}))=>json!("<redacted>"),
    12	        (Value::Array(xs),Some(ColumnType::List{of,..}|ColumnType::ListRange{of,..}))=>json!(xs.iter().map(|v|safe(v,Some(of))).collect::<Vec<_>>()),
    13	        (Value::String(_)|Value::Bytes(_)|Value::Row(_)|Value::Map(_)|Value::Array(_),None)=>json!("<redacted>"),
    14	        _=>serde_json::to_value(value).unwrap(),
    15	    }
    16	}
    17	fn image(bound:&Bound,table:&str,row:&snapback4_core::Value)->serde_json::Value {
    18	    let snapback4_core::Value::Row(fields)=row else{return json!("<redacted>");};
    19	    json!(fields.iter().map(|(name,value)|(name,safe(value,bound.schema.table(table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(name))))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>())
    20	}
    21	fn actual_args(c:&Counterexample)->&BTreeMap<String,snapback4_core::Value> {
    22	    if !c.args.is_empty(){return &c.args;}
    23	    c.history.iter().rev().find(|step|step.operation==c.operation&&step.viewer==c.viewer).map(|step|&step.args).unwrap_or(&c.args)
    24	}
    25	fn evidence(bound:&Bound,inv:&security::Invariant,c:&Counterexample)->serde_json::Value {
    26	    let args=actual_args(c).iter().map(|(name,value)|(name,safe(value,None))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    27	    let old=c.old.as_ref().map(|row|image(bound,&inv.table,row));let next=image(bound,&inv.table,&c.row);
    28	    let summary=format!("{}({}) as {} → ok; {} {} -> {}; candidate invariant {} only {} (finite generated-fixture replay, not proof)",c.operation,serde_json::to_string(&args).unwrap(),c.viewer,inv.target_label(),old.as_ref().map(|v|v.to_string()).unwrap_or_else(||"absent".into()),next,inv.target,inv.predicate);
    29	    let history=c.history.iter().map(|step|json!({"operation":step.operation,"viewer":step.viewer,"args":step.args.iter().map(|(k,v)|(k,safe(v,None))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(),"state":step.result["state"]})).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    30	    let supporting=c.supporting_rows.iter().map(|(table,rows)|(table,rows.iter().map(|r|image(bound,table,r)).collect::<Vec<_>>())).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    31	    json!({"history":history,"supportingRows":supporting,"summary":summary,"candidate":format!("invariant {} only {}",inv.target_label(),inv.predicate),"operation":c.operation,"viewer":c.viewer,"args":args,"effect":c.effect,"old":old,"row":next,"reachability":c.reachability,"scope":c.scope})
    32	}
    33	fn candidate(sources:&[(String,String)],line:&str)->Option<security::Invariant> {
    34	    let mut sources=sources.to_vec();sources.push(("$dx11-question.q".into(),format!("{line}\n")));
    35	    let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&sources);if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty(){return None;}
    36	    let mut inv=bound.invariants.into_iter().find(|i|i.site.file=="$dx11-question.q")?;
    37	    inv.site.file=format!("$derived/dx11/{}",hash(line.as_bytes()));Some(inv)
    38	}
    39	fn candidates(bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[Diagnostic])->Vec<(usize,security::Invariant)> {
    40	    let derived=security::derived::invariants(sources,bound);let mut out=Vec::new();
    41	    for (index,n) in notes.iter().enumerate() {
    42	        if n.code=="N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY" {for inv in &derived {if inv.site==n.site {out.push((index,inv.clone()));}}}
    43	        if n.code=="N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" {
    44	            if let Some(subject)=n.message.split(": ").nth(1).and_then(|s|s.split_whitespace().next()) {
    45	                for inv in &derived {if subject==format!("{}.{}",inv.table,inv.column.as_deref().unwrap_or("")) {out.push((index,inv.clone()));}}
    46	                if let Some(policy)=n.message.split("without the edit restriction of ").nth(1).and_then(|s|s.split_once(": ").map(|(_,p)|p)).and_then(|s|s.split("; creation admission:").next()) {
    47	                    if let Some(inv)=candidate(sources,&format!("invariant {subject} update only {policy}")) {out.push((index,inv));}
    48	                }
    49	            }
    50	        }
    51	        if n.code=="N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" {
    52	            let Some(subject)=n.message.split("reader-grant column ").nth(1) else{continue;};let Some((table,column))=subject.split_once('.') else{continue;};
    53	            // Name-matched role membership is a candidate question, never a rule.
    54	            for (roles,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables() {for (role,kind) in &t.columns {
    55	                if let ColumnType::Enum{variants}=kind.required() {if variants.iter().any(|v|v==column) {
    56	                    if let Some(principal)=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),ColumnType::Principal).then_some(c)) {
    57	                        if let Some(inv)=candidate(sources,&format!("invariant {table} always exists {roles}[{principal} = .{column}, {role} = '{column}']")) {out.push((index,inv));}
    58	                    }
    59	                }}
    60	            }}
    61	        }
    62	        if n.code=="N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED" {
    63	            let Some((subject,_))=n.message.split_once(':') else{continue;};let Some((table,column))=subject.split_once('.') else{continue;};let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table) else{continue;};
    64	            if !matches!(t.columns.get(column).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Bool)){continue;}
    65	            let stem=column.to_lowercase().split("approv").next().unwrap_or("").to_owned();
    66	            let trigger=t.columns.iter().find_map(|(c,k)|(c!=column&&!stem.is_empty()&&c.to_lowercase().starts_with(&stem)&&matches!(k.required(),ColumnType::Bool)).then_some(c));
    67	            let predicate=trigger.map(|trigger|format!("not .{trigger} or .{column}")).unwrap_or_else(||format!(".{column}"));
    68	            let Some(ops)=n.message.split("transitions ").nth(1).and_then(|s|s.split(" do not").next()) else{continue;};
    69	            // A column-change candidate uses the campaign's mature transition
    70	            // exploration; it observes the same operation, without adding a rule.
    71	            fn columns(value:&serde_json::Value,table:&str,out:&mut std::collections::BTreeSet<String>) {
    72	                if let Some(write)=value.get("WriteTarget").filter(|w|w["target"]["table"]==table) {
    73	                    if let Some(fields)=write["patch"]["Object"].as_object(){out.extend(fields.keys().cloned());}
    74	                }
    75	                if let Some(write)=value.get("Update").filter(|w|w["table"]==table) {
    76	                    if let Some(fields)=write["patch"]["Object"].as_object(){out.extend(fields.keys().cloned());}
    77	                }
    78	                match value {serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){columns(v,table,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{columns(v,table,out)},_=>{}}
    79	            }
    80	            for op in ops.split(", ").collect::<Vec<_>>().into_iter().rev() {
    81	                if let Some(program)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==op) {
    82	                    let mut changed=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();columns(&serde_json::to_value(&program.program).unwrap(),table,&mut changed);
    83	                    for col in changed.into_iter().filter(|c|matches!(t.columns.get(c).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Time))) {
    84	                        if let Some(inv)=candidate(sources,&format!("invariant {table}.{col} update only {predicate}")) {out.push((index,inv));}
    85	                    }
    86	                }
    87	            }
    88	        }
    89	    }out
    90	}
    91	/// Once an edit is admitted, try a concrete decreasing edit through that same
    92	/// operation. Both writes run on the engine; no fabricated old/new values.
    93	fn decreasing_edit(bound:&Bound,inv:&security::Invariant,c:&Counterexample)->Option<Counterexample> {
    94	    use snapback4_core::{Engine,Context,Value};
    95	    let column=inv.column.as_ref()?;
    96	    if !matches!(bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].columns.get(column)?.required(),ColumnType::Money{..}|ColumnType::Int|ColumnType::IntRange{..}|ColumnType::Decimal{..}) || !actual_args(c).contains_key(column) {return None;}

   117	/// Fixed work cap; generated cache never clears a gate or supplies authority.
   118	pub(in crate::verbs) fn collect(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[Diagnostic],expanded:bool)->Vec<Option<serde_json::Value>> {
   119	    let key=hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&("dx11-witness-5",sources,notes,record::capture(sources,None).ok())).unwrap());let path=project.schema_dir().join("generated/security-witnesses.json");
   120	    if let Some(cached)=std::fs::read(&path).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice::<serde_json::Value>(&b).ok()) {if cached["key"]==key {if let Ok(values)=serde_json::from_value(cached["values"].clone()){return values;}}}
   121	    if !expanded {return vec![None;notes.len()];}
   122	    let selected=candidates(bound,sources,notes);let mut result=vec![None;notes.len()];
   123	    if !selected.is_empty() {
   124	        let mut questions=bound.clone();questions.invariants=selected.iter().map(|(_,i)|i.clone()).collect();
   125	        let mut campaign=challenges::Campaign{work_remaining:Some(8192),..Default::default()};challenges::generated_campaign(&questions,&mut campaign,false);
   126	        for (index,inv) in &selected {
   127	            if result[*index].is_some(){continue;}
   128	            if let Some(c)=campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&c.reachability.is_reachable()).max_by_key(|c| {
   129	                let decrease=inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|column|Some((c.old.as_ref()?.field(column).ok()?,c.row.field(column).ok()?))).is_some_and(|(old,next)|matches!((old,next),(snapback4_core::Value::Int(a),snapback4_core::Value::Int(b)) if a>b));
   130	                (decrease,c.history.len())
   131	            }) {let replay=decreasing_edit(bound,inv,c);result[*index]=Some(evidence(bound,inv,replay.as_ref().unwrap_or(c)));}
   132	        }
   133	    }
   134	    let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
   135	    if std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec(&json!({"key":key,"values":result})).unwrap()).is_ok(){let _=std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path);}let _=std::fs::remove_file(temporary);result
   136	}

//! Wave-28 decision commands, concise success and witness records.
use std::{fs,path::PathBuf,process::Command};
use serde_json::Value;
struct Scratch(PathBuf);impl Drop for Scratch{fn drop(&mut self){let _=fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
fn scratch()->Scratch{static NEXT:std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize=std::sync::atomic::AtomicUsize::new(0);let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-dx11-{}-{}",std::process::id(),NEXT.fetch_add(1,std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed)));fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback")).unwrap();Scratch(root)}
fn run(p:&Scratch,args:&[&str])->std::process::Output{Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4")).current_dir(&p.0).args(args).output().unwrap()}
fn json(p:&Scratch,args:&[&str])->Value{let out=run(p,&[&["check","--json"][..],args].concat());serde_json::from_slice(&out.stdout).unwrap_or_else(|_|panic!("{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr)))}
const SOURCE:&str="table docs:\n  owner:principal\n  body:text <=100\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(body:text <=100):\n  return insert docs {owner:viewer,body}\nquery unrelated():\n  return true\n";
#[test]fn passing_check_hides_advisory_details_but_keeps_enforcement_and_next_command(){
 let p=scratch();fs::write(p.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),SOURCE).unwrap();
 let out=run(&p,&["check"]);assert!(out.status.success());let text=format!("{}{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout),String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));assert!(text.lines().count()<=5,"{text}");assert!(text.contains("check --review")&&text.contains("enforced by the language"),"{text}");assert!(!text.contains("decision N_")&&!text.contains("is this intended?"),"{text}");
 let expanded=run(&p,&["check","--review"]);let text=String::from_utf8_lossy(&expanded.stdout);assert!(expanded.status.success());assert!(text.contains("Intended: snapback4 check --ack-security"),"{text}");
}
#[test]fn every_json_id_accepts_atomic_repeated_and_comma_syntax_and_unrelated_edits(){
 let p=scratch();let source=p.0.join("snapback/schema.q");fs::write(&source,SOURCE).unwrap();let first=json(&p,&[]);let ids=first["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().map(|d|d["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>();assert!(ids.len()>=2);
 let out=run(&p,&["check","--ack-security",&ids[0],"--ack-security","unknown","--reason","explicit specification rationale"]);assert_eq!(out.status.code(),Some(2));assert!(!p.0.join("snapback/security-reviews.json").exists());assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).contains("stale"));
 let comma=ids[1..].join(",");let out=run(&p,&["check","--ack-security",&ids[0],"--ack-security",&comma,"--reason","explicit specification rationale"]);assert!(out.status.success(),"{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
 fs::write(&source,SOURCE.replace("return true","return false")).unwrap();let after=json(&p,&[]);assert!(after["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().all(|d|d["acknowledged"]==true),"{after}");
}
#[test]fn wave28_false_premise_decisions_retain_the_concrete_witness(){
 for (source,codes) in [(include_str!("fixtures/dx11/011.q"),vec!["N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED","N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"]),(include_str!("fixtures/dx11/019.q"),vec!["N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT"])] {
  let p=scratch();fs::write(p.0.join("snapback/schema.q"),source).unwrap();let report=json(&p,&[]);
  for code in codes {
   let decision=report["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().find(|d|d["code"]==code&&d["witness"].is_object()&&(code!="N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"||d["witness"]["row"]["homeowner"].is_object())).unwrap_or_else(||panic!("missing witness for {code}"));
   let witness=&decision["witness"];
   if code=="N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED" {assert_eq!(witness["operation"],"recordInstallation");assert_eq!(witness["row"]["indoorMount"]["Bool"],true);assert_eq!(witness["row"]["indoorApproved"]["Bool"],false);}
   if code=="N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" {assert_eq!(witness["operation"],"setAccount");assert_eq!(witness["effect"],"update");assert!(witness["args"].get("balance").is_some());assert!(witness["old"]["balance"]["Int"].as_i64().unwrap()>witness["row"]["balance"]["Int"].as_i64().unwrap());}
   let id=decision["id"].as_str().unwrap();let result=json(&p,&["--ack-security",id,"--reason","The specification explicitly permits this witnessed behavior"]);assert_eq!(result["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().find(|d|d["id"]==id).unwrap()["acknowledged"],true);
   let recorded:Value=serde_json::from_slice(&fs::read(p.0.join("snapback/security-reviews.json")).unwrap()).unwrap();assert_eq!(recorded["entries"][id]["witness"],*witness);
  }
 }
}

 assert!(!out.status.success());let public=String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);let private=String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
 assert!(public.contains("E_RULE"),"{public} {private}");assert!(!public.contains("column write grant")&&!public.contains("write <-"),"{public}");assert!(private.contains("column write grant body")&&private.contains("body:text <=100 write <-"),"{private}");
}

//! Positive campaign evidence only; never a cached security proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — exact source/fixture/executable binding.
use super::*;
use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};
#[derive(serde::Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub(super) struct Record{
    #[serde(default)] pub recorded_at:Option<i64>,
    #[serde(default)] pub last_completed_at:Option<i64>,
    #[serde(default)] pub run_violations:Option<usize>,
    #[serde(default)] pub directed_attempts:usize,#[serde(default)] pub directed_peak_worlds:usize,
    pub fingerprint:String,pub complete:bool,pub attempted:usize,pub sequences:usize,pub coverage_gaps:usize,pub violations:Vec<security::challenges::Counterexample>}
pub(super) fn selected_fixture(project:&Project,options:&Options)->std::path::PathBuf{
    let flag=if options.flags.contains_key("source"){"source"}else{"schema"};
    options.flags.get("fixture").map(std::path::PathBuf::from).unwrap_or_else(||{
        let saved=options.flags.get(flag).map(std::path::Path::new).map(|p|if p.is_dir(){p.join("fixtures")}else if p.file_name().is_some_and(|n|n=="schema.q"){p.parent().unwrap().join("fixtures")}else{p.with_file_name(format!("{}-fixtures",p.file_stem().unwrap_or_default().to_string_lossy()))});
        saved.filter(|p|p.is_dir()).unwrap_or_else(||project.root.join("fixtures"))
    })
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn capture(sources:&[(String,String)],fixture:Option<&std::path::Path>)->Result<String,String>{
    let mut hash=Sha256::new();hash.update(b"SEC4-test-record-v2/full-default/NOW=1800000000000");
    for (name,text) in sources{hash.update((name.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(name);hash.update((text.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(text);}
    let binary=std::env::current_exe().and_then(std::fs::read).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;hash.update(Sha256::digest(binary));
    if let Some(path)=fixture.filter(|p|p.is_dir()){
        let mut files=std::fs::read_dir(path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;files.sort_by_key(|e|e.file_name());
        for f in files{
            // Follow the same regular-file symlinks as the fixture loader.
            if !std::fs::metadata(f.path()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_file(){continue;}
            let name=f.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned();let bytes=std::fs::read(f.path()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            hash.update((name.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(name);hash.update((bytes.len()as u64).to_le_bytes());hash.update(bytes);
        }
    }Ok(format!("{:x}",hash.finalize()))
}
fn fingerprint(project:&Project,options:&Options)->Result<String,String>{
    let flag=if options.flags.contains_key("source"){"source"}else{"schema"};
    capture(&super::super::proof_source::sources(project,options,flag)?,Some(&selected_fixture(project,options)))
}
fn path(project:&Project)->std::path::PathBuf{project.root.join("snapback/generated/security-test.json")}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn remember(project:&Project,options:&Options,campaign:&security::challenges::Campaign,complete:bool,consumed:&str)->Result<(),String>{
### 17.2 Campaign honesty and useful generated roots

Every invariant outside the finite evaluator's fragment is named by target and
source site in default test output with its reason; a separate evaluation-gap
count prevents zero violations being read as full invariant coverage. Static
unproved remains unproved. No unknown evaluation becomes policy false. Inherited
raw read-through child assertions remain explicit evaluation gaps; this amendment
does not add raw evaluator capability. Future support requires §8.1 seed ingress,
committed readback, parent lifetime/retirement and final-exists validation. A
snapshot-only comparison never sets refuted.
Generated required uploads use real verified bytes and actor-owned fresh ledger
entries, satisfying declared byte limits; no argument/type/admission bypass.
Generated creators reserve at most two rows per target table. After an admitted
creator tuple, try a tuple differing in at least one boolean before another actor
can consume the second row slot; this is not complete Cartesian coverage. Keep
the existing 16 tuple/2048 creator-call initialization limits and report omitted
boolean combinations. Directed exploration uses depth 12, 4096 aggregate attempts,
eight argument tuples per operation/world, three distinct successors per operation,
and a five-world beam (at most 16 retained worlds including current/next/successors).
These bounds are shared, not multiplied per creator boolean. Breadth remains 512.
Check retains its shared 512-work/1500ms cap; no new cap confers proof. Report every
cut and retain generated provenance.

### 17.3 Presentation and review identity

Human output collapses restatement and role-wide questions into one questionnaire

//! Individually justified source-bound dispositions; never proof or authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#162-narrow-gates-and-individual-dispositions — bulk compiler-note review cannot clear security gates.
use super::*;
use serde::{Serialize,Deserialize};
use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};
const ANALYZER:&str="sec11-1";
mod binding;
mod decisions;
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct File{version:u32,entries:BTreeMap<String,Entry>}
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Entry{sources:String,analyzer:String,reason:String,#[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")] witness:Option<serde_json::Value>}
#[derive(Clone,Serialize)]
pub(super) struct Disposition{pub family:Option<String>,pub id:String,pub code:String,pub site:String,pub gated:bool,pub acknowledged:bool,pub recorded:bool,pub reason:Option<String>,pub scope:String,pub witness:Option<serde_json::Value>}
pub(super) fn gated(code:&str)->bool{snapback4_lang::security::gated_note(code)}
fn hash(bytes:&[u8])->String{format!("{:x}",Sha256::digest(bytes))}
fn digest(s:&str)->bool{s.len()==64&&s.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_hexdigit())}
pub(super) fn review(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>)->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    review_inner(project,sources,notes,ack,false,&[])
}
/// Read-only decisions for a caller-validated immutable saved-source selection.
pub(super) fn review_snapshot(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic])->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    review_inner(project,sources,notes,None,true,&[])
}
pub(super) fn review_evidence(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>,evidence:&[Option<serde_json::Value>])->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    review_inner(project,sources,notes,ack,false,evidence)
}
fn review_inner(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>,snapshot:bool,evidence:&[Option<serde_json::Value>])->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    if !snapshot&&project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed during security review; rerun check".into());}
    let identities=binding::identities(notes,sources);
    let path=project.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");
    // Keep the lock inode in ignored generated output. Do not unlink a lock
    // while another process can be waiting on that inode. Kernel ownership
    // releases it on exit, including an interrupted writer.
    let _writer_lock=if ack.is_some(){
        let directory=project.schema_dir().join("generated");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&directory).map_err(|e|format!("cannot create security review lock directory: {e}"))?;
        let lock=std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).truncate(false).read(true).write(true).open(directory.join("security-reviews.lock")).map_err(|e|format!("cannot open security review lock: {e}"))?;
        lock.lock().map_err(|e|format!("cannot acquire security review lock: {e}"))?;Some(lock)
    }else{None};
    if !snapshot&&project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed while waiting for security review; rerun check".into());}
    let mut file:File=match std::fs::read(&path){
        Ok(bytes)=>serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_|"invalid snapback/security-reviews.json; expected version 1 with per-note source/analyzer/reason records".to_string())?,
        Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound=>File{version:1,entries:BTreeMap::new()},
        Err(e)=>return Err(format!("cannot read security dispositions: {e}")),
    };
    if file.version!=1||file.entries.iter().any(|(key,e)|!digest(key)||!digest(&e.sources)||e.reason.trim().is_empty()||e.analyzer.is_empty()){return Err("invalid security disposition version/digest/reason".into());}
    let mut recorded=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    if let Some((key,reason))=ack{
        if reason.trim().is_empty(){return Err("--reason needs the specification rationale for this individual note".into());}
        // Validate the complete batch against the same captured source/note set.
        // Nothing is published when even one member is stale or unknown.
        let mut selected=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        for key in key.split(',').map(str::trim) {
            if key.starts_with("audience:") {
                let Some(family)=decisions::family(notes,&identities) else {return Err("no current advisory audience family; run check --review --json".into());};
                if family==key {
                    selected.extend(notes.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_,note)|decisions::audience(&note.code)).map(|(i,_)|i));
                } else {
                    let history: BTreeMap<String,Vec<(String,String)>>=std::fs::read(project.schema_dir().join("generated/security-families.json")).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
                    let members=history.get(key).ok_or("stale or unknown audience family: rerun check --review; existing individual decisions are preserved")?;
                    for (id,fingerprint) in members {
                        let index=identities.iter().enumerate().find(|(i,(current,binding,_))|current==id&&binding==fingerprint&&decisions::audience(&notes[*i].code)).map(|(i,_)|i).ok_or("stale audience family member: a relevant rule changed or disappeared; no decisions recorded; run check --review")?;
                        selected.insert(index);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                let index=identities.iter().position(|(id,_,_)|id==key).ok_or_else(||format!("--ack-security {key} is stale, belongs to another source selection, or is not a decision ID. Use securityReviews[].id from `check --review --json` (not a diagnostic code or invariant site); repeated --ack-security ID or comma-separated IDs share one --reason"))?;
                selected.insert(index);
            }
        }
        recorded=selected.clone();
        for index in selected {
            let (id,fingerprint,_)=&identities[index];
            file.entries.insert(id.clone(),Entry{sources:fingerprint.clone(),analyzer:ANALYZER.into(),reason:reason.trim().into(),witness:evidence.get(index).cloned().flatten()});
        }
        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("json.{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
        let result=std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&file).unwrap()).and_then(|_|{
            if project.sources()?!=sources{return Err(std::io::Error::other("Quarry sources changed before disposition publication; rerun check"));}
            std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path)
        });
        if let Err(e)=result{let _=std::fs::remove_file(&temporary);return Err(format!("cannot record security disposition: {e}"));}
    }
    let family=decisions::family(notes,&identities);
    if !snapshot && let Some(family)=&family {
        let path=project.schema_dir().join("generated/security-families.json");
        let mut history:BTreeMap<String,Vec<(String,String)>>=std::fs::read(&path).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
        history.insert(family.clone(),identities.iter().zip(notes).filter(|(_,n)|decisions::audience(&n.code)).map(|((id,binding,_),_)|(id.clone(),binding.clone())).collect());
        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
        let _=std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap());
        if std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec(&history).unwrap()).is_ok(){let _=std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path);}let _=std::fs::remove_file(temporary);
    }
    Ok(notes.iter().zip(identities).enumerate().map(|(i,(n,(key,fingerprint,scope)))|{let entry=file.entries.get(&key).filter(|e|e.sources==fingerprint&&e.analyzer==ANALYZER);
        Disposition{family:decisions::audience(&n.code).then(||family.clone()).flatten(),id:key,code:n.code.clone(),site:n.site.to_string(),gated:gated(&n.code),acknowledged:entry.is_some(),recorded:recorded.contains(&i),scope,reason:entry.map(|e|e.reason.clone()),witness:entry.and_then(|e|e.witness.clone()).or_else(||evidence.get(i).cloned().flatten())}
    }).collect())
}
pub(super) fn pending(dispositions:&[Disposition])->bool{dispositions.iter().any(|d|d.gated&&!d.acknowledged)}
pub(super) use decisions::{print,render,render_mode,count as decision_count};
#[cfg(test)]mod tests{
use super::*;
pub(super) struct Scratch(pub(super) std::path::PathBuf);impl Drop for Scratch{fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
#[test]fn individual_source_and_diagnostic_bound_dispositions(){
 let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-sec7-dispositions-{}",std::process::id()));std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback")).unwrap();
 let _scratch=Scratch(root.clone());let p=Project{root:root.clone()};std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),"query q():\n  return true\n").unwrap();let sources=p.sources().unwrap();
 let a=snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","a","");let b=snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT","b","");let notes=[a.clone(),b];let key=binding::identities(&notes,&sources)[0].0.clone();
 assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&key,"  "))).is_err());assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some(("unknown","intended"))).is_err());
 let ds=review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&key,"the specification allows optional draft evidence"))).unwrap();assert!(ds[0].acknowledged);assert!(!ds[1].acknowledged);assert!(pending(&ds));
 let changed=[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","changed","")];assert!(!review(&p,&sources,&changed,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);
 let path=p.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");let mut f:File=serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(&path).unwrap()).unwrap();for old in ["sec7-2","sec7-3","sec7-4","sec7-5","sec7-6","sec7-7","sec7-8","sec7-9","sec9-1","sec9-3"]{f.entries.get_mut(&key).unwrap().analyzer=old.into();std::fs::write(&path,serde_json::to_vec(&f).unwrap()).unwrap();assert!(!review(&p,&sources,&notes,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);}
 std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),"query q():\n  return false\n").unwrap();assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&key,"intended"))).is_err());assert!(!review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);
 std::fs::write(&path,"{}").unwrap();assert!(review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,None).is_err());std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
}
#[test]fn repeated_advisories_are_individually_reviewed_and_survive_comments(){
 let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-sec8-advisory-{}",std::process::id()));std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback")).unwrap();let _scratch=Scratch(root.clone());let p=Project{root};
 let text="query q():\n  return true\n";std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),text).unwrap();
 let mut n=snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&snapback4_lang::Site::new("schema.q",2,3),"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ","q.read: reviewed audience","confirm spec");let notes=vec![n.clone(),n.clone()];
 let ds=review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,None).unwrap();assert_ne!(ds[0].id,ds[1].id);
 let ds=review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,Some((&ds[0].id,"Specification allows this audience"))).unwrap();assert!(ds[0].acknowledged);assert!(!ds[1].acknowledged);assert!(!pending(&ds));
 std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),format!("-- comment\n{text}")).unwrap();n.site.line+=1;
 let ds=review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&[n.clone(),n],None).unwrap();assert!(ds[0].acknowledged);assert!(!ds[1].acknowledged);

#[cfg(test)] mod family_tests {
 use super::*;
 #[test] fn audience_command_records_current_members_but_never_gates_or_changed_families() {
  let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-dx9-family-{}",std::process::id()));
  std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback")).unwrap();let _scratch=super::tests::Scratch(root.clone());
  let p=Project{root};std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),"query q():\n  return true\n").unwrap();let sources=p.sources().unwrap();
  let n=|code:&str,message:&str|snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),code,message,"confirm");
  let notes=vec![n("N_ROLE_WIDE_READ","notes.read: audience A"),n("N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE","notes.write: audience B"),n("N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING","gate")];
  let ds=review(&p,&sources,&notes,None).unwrap();let family=ds[0].family.clone().unwrap();assert_eq!(ds[1].family.as_ref(),Some(&family));assert!(ds[2].family.is_none());
  let recorded=review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&family,"The specification admits both audiences"))).unwrap();
  assert!(recorded[0].acknowledged&&recorded[1].acknowledged);assert!(!recorded[2].acknowledged);assert!(pending(&recorded));
  let mut changed=notes.clone();changed.push(n("N_BROAD_READ","extra.read: another audience"));
  let retained=review(&p,&sources,&changed,Some((&family,"old decision"))).unwrap();
  assert!(retained[0].acknowledged&&retained[1].acknowledged);assert!(!retained[2].acknowledged&&!retained[3].acknowledged);
  assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&family,"  "))).is_err());
 }
}
//! A family command expands to current individual, source-bound records.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#18-wave-26-security-decisions-dx9 — review questions lead, and gates never enter the audience family.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::Diagnostic;

pub(super) fn audience(code: &str) -> bool {
    !gated(code) && matches!(code, "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE" | "N_BROAD_READ")
}
pub(super) fn family(notes: &[Diagnostic], identities: &[(String,String,String)]) -> Option<String> {
    let mut members = notes.iter().zip(identities).filter(|(note,_)| audience(&note.code))
        .map(|(_, (id,source,_))| (id,source)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if members.is_empty() { return None; }
    members.sort();
    Some(format!("audience:{}", hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&(ANALYZER,members)).unwrap())))
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn count(dispositions: &[Disposition]) -> usize {
    let audience = dispositions.iter().any(|d| !d.acknowledged && d.family.is_some());
    usize::from(audience) + dispositions.iter().filter(|d| !d.acknowledged && d.family.is_none()
        && crate::note_rank::tier(&d.code)==crate::note_rank::Tier::Review).count()
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn print(dispositions: &[Disposition], notes: &[Diagnostic]) {print!("{}",render(dispositions,notes));}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn render(dispositions: &[Disposition], notes: &[Diagnostic]) -> String {
    use std::fmt::Write;
    let mut output=String::new();
    let mut ordered=notes.iter().zip(dispositions).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#165-review-clarification-exact-detector-and-command-boundaries — grouping must preserve gate/authority priority.
    ordered.sort_by_key(|(n,_)|(crate::note_rank::summary_order(&n.code),n.site.file.clone(),n.site.line,n.site.col));
    let members=ordered.iter().filter(|(_,d)|d.family.is_some()&&!d.acknowledged).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut family_output=String::new();
    if let Some((_,first))=members.first().copied() {
        let codes=members.iter().map(|(n,_)|n.code.as_str()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"decision audience ({codes}): Are these {} row/column audiences, roles and lifecycle states intended?",members.len()).unwrap();
        for (n,_) in &members {
            // The expanded branch cross-product stays in JSON. One question
            // names the source subject without repeating every equivalent path.
            let subject = n.message.split(" matches or weakens").next().unwrap_or(&n.message)
                .split(": ").next().unwrap_or(&n.message);
            writeln!(&mut family_output,"  ? {}: Is {} intended?",n.site,subject.trim_end_matches('.')).unwrap();
        }
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"  Intended: snapback4 check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'",first.family.as_deref().unwrap()).unwrap();
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"  Narrower: restrict the listed rules; declare independent intent; run snapback4 test. Full audiences: check --json.").unwrap();
    }
    let mut family_printed=false;
    for (n,d) in ordered {
        if d.acknowledged {if let Some(w)=&d.witness {writeln!(&mut output,"  Witness recorded: {}",w["summary"].as_str().unwrap_or("see check --json")).unwrap();} writeln!(&mut output,"security reviewed {} {}: {}",d.id,d.code,d.reason.as_deref().unwrap_or("")).unwrap(); continue; }
        if d.family.is_some() {
            if !family_printed {output.push_str(&family_output);family_printed=true;}
            continue;
        }
        if !d.gated && crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review { continue; }
        let subject=n.message.split(": ").next().unwrap_or(&n.message);
        let concise=match n.code.as_str() {
            "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE"=>format!("{subject}: should operation results/refusals expose protected or projected-out columns to every admitted caller"),
            _=>n.message.trim_end_matches(['.', '?']).to_owned(),
        };
        let concise=concise.replace("insert/upsert", "insert, or edit an existing row via upsert");
        let question=concise.trim_end_matches(['.', '?']);
        writeln!(&mut output,"decision {} at {}: {question}; is this intended?",d.code,d.site).unwrap();
        if let Some(witness)=&d.witness {writeln!(&mut output,"  Witness: {}",witness["summary"].as_str().unwrap_or("concrete campaign behavior; check --json for replay")).unwrap();}
        else if matches!(n.code.as_str(),"N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED"|"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"|"N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT"|"N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY") {writeln!(&mut output,"  Witness: none obtained in this bounded campaign; `check --review` searches for concrete behavior; this remains a specification question").unwrap();}
        let repair=match n.code.as_str() {
            "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE"=>"restrict result/refusal audiences with column rules and independent intent; run snapback4 test",
            "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"=>"validate the recipient's current role and relationship; caller authority alone is insufficient",
            _=>n.rewrite.as_str(),
        };
        writeln!(&mut output,"  Repair: {repair}").unwrap();
        writeln!(&mut output,"  Intended: snapback4 check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'{}",d.id,if d.gated {" (required before check can pass)"}else{""}).unwrap();
    }
    output
}

/// One mode selector for the CLI and captured-analyzer format replays.
pub(in crate::verbs) fn render_mode(dispositions:&[Disposition],notes:&[Diagnostic],review:bool)->String {
    let selected=dispositions.iter().zip(notes).filter(|(d,_)|review || d.recorded || d.gated&&!d.acknowledged).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    render(&selected.iter().map(|(d,_)|(*d).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>(),&selected.iter().map(|(_,n)|(*n).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>())
}
#[cfg(test)]mod tests {
 use super::*;
 #[test]fn authority_and_gates_precede_one_complete_audience_family(){
  let codes=["N_BROAD_READ","N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION","N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"];
  let notes=codes.iter().map(|code|Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),*code,"subject: review authority","repair enforcement")).collect::<Vec<_>>();
  let dispositions=codes.iter().map(|code|Disposition{family:audience(code).then(||"audience:family".into()),id:(*code).into(),code:(*code).into(),site:"schema.q:1:1".into(),gated:gated(code),acknowledged:false,recorded:false,reason:None,witness:None,scope:"test".into()}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
  let text=render(&dispositions,&notes);
  assert!(text.find("decision N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE").unwrap()<text.find("decision N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION").unwrap(),"{text}");
  assert!(text.find("decision N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION").unwrap()<text.find("decision audience").unwrap(),"{text}");
  assert_eq!(text.matches("decision audience").count(),1,"{text}");
  assert!(text.contains("Are these 2 row/column audiences")&&text.contains("audience:family"),"{text}");
 }
 #[test]fn every_gate_is_one_decision_and_never_a_family_member(){
  for code in snapback4_lang::security::GATED_NOTE_CODES {
   assert!(!audience(code));
   let n=Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),*code,"explicit review required","repair enforcement");
   let d=Disposition{family:None,id:"id".into(),code:(*code).into(),site:"schema.q:1:1".into(),gated:true,acknowledged:false,recorded:false,reason:None,witness:None,scope:"test".into()};
   let text=render(&[d],&[n]);assert_eq!(text.matches(code).count(),1,"{text}");assert!(text.contains("is this intended?")&&text.contains("Repair:")&&text.contains("Intended:"));
  }
  for code in ["N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY","N_CALLER_TIME","N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE","N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED","E_SECURITY_REVIEW"] {assert!(!audience(code));}
 }
}

#[cfg(test)]mod sec10_replay {
 use super::*;
 fn diagnostic(n:&serde_json::Value)->Diagnostic {Diagnostic::new(&snapback4_lang::Site::new(n["site"]["file"].as_str().unwrap(),n["site"]["line"].as_u64().unwrap() as u32,n["site"]["col"].as_u64().unwrap() as u32),n["code"].as_str().unwrap(),n["message"].as_str().unwrap(),n["rewrite"].as_str().unwrap())}
 fn disposition(n:&Diagnostic,gated:bool)->Disposition {Disposition{family:None,id:n.code.clone(),code:n.code.clone(),site:n.site.to_string(),gated,acknowledged:false,recorded:false,reason:None,scope:"captured SEC10 source-bound decision".into(),witness:None}}
 #[test]fn captured_sec10_codes_keep_every_gate_and_hide_advisories_by_default(){
  let codes:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../../tests/fixtures/dx11/sec10-codes.json")).unwrap();
  for n in codes.as_object().unwrap().values(){let n=diagnostic(n);let d=disposition(&n,false);assert!(render_mode(&[d],&[n.clone()],false).is_empty());let d=disposition(&n,true);let output=render_mode(&[d],&[n.clone()],false);assert!(output.contains(&n.code)&&output.contains("Intended:"),"{output}");}
 }
 /// Opt-in measurement on all 50 actual SEC10 structured reports. No analyzer
 /// or source is modified: only the production presentation function runs.
 #[test]fn captured_sec10_measurement(){
  let Ok(path)=std::env::var("SNAPBACK4_DX11_SEC10_REPORTS")else{return;};
  let rows:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(path).unwrap()).unwrap();let mut measured=Vec::new();
  for row in rows.as_array().unwrap(){
   let notes=row["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().map(diagnostic).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   let ds=row["dispositions"].as_array().unwrap().iter().zip(&notes).map(|(d,n)|disposition(n,d["gated"]==true)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   let gate_count=ds.iter().filter(|d|d.gated).count();
   let project=crate::project::Project{root:std::path::PathBuf::from(format!("/tmp/dx11-replay/sec10/{}",row["lane"].as_str().unwrap()))};
   let sources=project.sources().unwrap();let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&sources);assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty());
   let mut report=snapback4_lang::security::analyze_rules(&bound);report.notes=notes.clone();
   let advice=ds.iter().filter(|d|!d.gated&&crate::note_rank::tier(&d.code)==crate::note_rank::Tier::Review).count();
   let verdict=crate::presentation::check_verdict(gate_count>0,&crate::verbs::table_counts(&bound.schema),bound.schema.synced().count(),bound.programs.len(),if gate_count==0{"compiled"}else{"security review required"},gate_count,advice,0,row["noteCount"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0) as usize,row["ms"].as_f64().unwrap(),crate::verbs::init::client_setup_hint(&project.root),"");
   let text=format!("{verdict}\n{}\n{}",crate::verbs::security::enforced_text(&report,&bound),render_mode(&ds,&notes,false));
   let lines=text.lines().count();if gate_count==0{assert!(lines<=5);}
   measured.push(serde_json::json!({"lane":row["lane"],"exit":if gate_count==0{0}else{1},"lines":lines,"chars":text.chars().count(),"gates":gate_count}));
  }
  if let Ok(path)=std::env::var("SNAPBACK4_DX11_RENDER_OUTPUT"){std::fs::write(path,serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&measured).unwrap()).unwrap();}
 }
}

//! Stable semantic review bindings. Complex flow notes conservatively bind
//! their declaration dependency/writer closure, never the whole source set.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#173-presentation-and-review-identity — review survives independent edits.
use super::*;
use std::collections::BTreeSet;
use snapback4_lang::lexer::{lex,Kind};
#[derive(Clone)]
struct Declaration{file:String,line:u32,end:u32,name:String,kind:String,tokens:Vec<(String,String)>}
fn declarations(sources:&[(String,String)])->Vec<Declaration>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for (file,text) in sources{
        let (tokens,_)=lex(file,text);
        let starts=tokens.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_,t)|t.site.col==1&&t.kind==Kind::Word).map(|(i,_)|i).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        for (n,start) in starts.iter().enumerate(){
            let end=starts.get(n+1).copied().unwrap_or(tokens.len());let ts=&tokens[*start..end];
            let Some(name)=ts.get(1).filter(|t|t.kind==Kind::Word)else{continue;};
            out.push(Declaration{file:file.clone(),line:ts[0].site.line,end:tokens.get(end).map_or(u32::MAX,|t|t.site.line),name:name.value.clone(),kind:ts[0].value.clone(),tokens:ts.iter().filter(|t|!matches!(t.kind,Kind::Newline|Kind::Eof)).map(|t|(format!("{:?}",t.kind),t.value.clone())).collect()});
        }
    }
    out
}
fn words(text:&str)->BTreeSet<&str>{text.split(|c:char|!c.is_alphanumeric()&&c!='_').filter(|s|!s.is_empty()).collect()}
fn stable_message(text:&str)->String{
    // Source sites in detector prose are evidence, not review identity.
    text.split_inclusive(char::is_whitespace).map(|chunk|{
        let word=chunk.trim_end_matches(char::is_whitespace);
        let suffix=&chunk[word.len()..];
        let parts=word.split(':').collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let normalized=if parts.len()>=3&&parts[parts.len()-2].parse::<u32>().is_ok()&&parts.last().unwrap().trim_end_matches([',',';',')']).parse::<u32>().is_ok(){parts[..parts.len()-2].join(":")}else{word.into()};
        format!("{normalized}{suffix}")
    }).collect()

}
fn strip_sites(value:&mut serde_json::Value){match value{
    serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>{
        let metadata=fields.get("site").is_some_and(|site|site.is_string()||site.as_object().is_some_and(|site|site.len()==3&&site.contains_key("file")&&site.contains_key("line")&&site.contains_key("col")));
        if metadata{fields.remove("site");}
        for value in fields.values_mut(){strip_sites(value);}
    },
    serde_json::Value::Array(values)=>for value in values{strip_sites(value)},_=>{}
}}
pub(super) fn identities(notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],sources:&[(String,String)])->Vec<(String,String,String)>{
    let decls=declarations(sources);
    let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(sources);
    let mut occurrences=BTreeMap::<String,usize>::new();
    notes.iter().map(|note|{
        let owner=decls.iter().find(|d|d.file==note.site.file&&d.line<=note.site.line&&note.site.line<d.end);
        let subject=owner.map(|d|format!("{}:{}:{}",d.file,d.kind,d.name)).unwrap_or_else(||note.site.file.clone());
        let base=hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&(ANALYZER,&note.code,&subject,stable_message(&note.message),stable_message(&note.rewrite))).unwrap());
        let ordinal=occurrences.entry(base.clone()).or_default();let key=hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&(&base,*ordinal)).unwrap());*ordinal+=1;
        let mentioned=words(&note.message);
        let mut selected=decls.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_,d)|mentioned.contains(d.name.as_str())||owner.is_some_and(|o|std::ptr::eq(o,*d))).map(|(i,_)|i).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        // Optional-column advice is exactly about that declaration's type and
        // rule; unrelated columns in the same table are not dependencies.
        let column=note.message.split_whitespace().next().and_then(|s|s.split_once('.')).filter(|(_,c)|!c.contains(':'));
        let precise=if note.code=="N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE"{column.and_then(|(table,column)|{
            sources.iter().find_map(|(file,text)|{
                let (doc,_)=snapback4_lang::parse_document(file,text);
                let c=doc.tables.iter().find(|t|t.name==table)?.columns.iter().find(|c|c.name==column)?;
                let (tokens,_)=lex(file,text);let tokens=tokens.iter().filter(|t|t.site.line==c.site.line&&!matches!(t.kind,Kind::Newline|Kind::Eof)).map(|t|(format!("{:?}",t.kind),t.value.clone())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                Some(serde_json::to_vec(&(file,table,column,tokens)).unwrap())
            })
        })}else{None};
        let rule_binding=if matches!(note.code.as_str(),"N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"|"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"|"N_BROAD_READ"){
            let label=note.message.split_whitespace().next().unwrap_or("").trim_end_matches(':');
            label.split_once('.').and_then(|(table,part)|{
                let t=bound.schema.tables.get(table)?;
                let column=t.columns.contains_key(part).then_some(part);
                let effect=if column.is_some(){note.message.split_whitespace().nth(1).unwrap_or("read").trim_end_matches(':')}else{part};
                let mut columns=column.into_iter().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
                if note.code=="N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"&&let Some(group)=note.message.split("protected columns [").nth(1).and_then(|s|s.split(']').next()){
                    columns.extend(group.split(',').map(str::trim).filter(|c|t.columns.contains_key(*c)));
                }
                let column_bindings=columns.iter().map(|c|(*c,serde_json::json!({"type":t.columns.get(*c),"read":t.column_reads.get(*c),"owner":t.written_by.get(*c)}))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
                let mut value=serde_json::json!({"rowRead":t.rules.read,"columns":column_bindings,"effect":effect,
                    "invariants":bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==table&&(i.column.is_none()&&column.is_none()||i.column.as_deref().is_some_and(|c|columns.contains(c)))&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write")).collect::<Vec<_>>()});
                if effect!="read"{value["writeRules"]=serde_json::to_value(&t.rules).unwrap();value["owners"]=serde_json::to_value(&t.written_by).unwrap();}
                strip_sites(&mut value);
                let encoded=value.to_string();
                let mut dependencies=bound.schema.tables.keys().filter(|name|name.as_str()!=table&&encoded.contains(&format!("\"{name}\""))).cloned().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
                loop{let old=dependencies.clone();for name in &old{let serialized=serde_json::to_string(&bound.schema.tables[name]).unwrap();for candidate in bound.schema.tables.keys(){if candidate.as_str()!=table&&serialized.contains(&format!("\"{candidate}\"")){dependencies.insert(candidate.clone());}}}if dependencies==old{break;}}
                value["dependencies"]=serde_json::to_value(dependencies.iter().map(|name|(name,&bound.schema.tables[name])).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>()).unwrap();
                let mut writers=serde_json::to_value(bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(table,_)|dependencies.contains(table))).map(|p|&p.program).collect::<Vec<_>>()).unwrap();strip_sites(&mut writers);value["dependencyWriters"]=writers;
                Some(serde_json::to_vec(&value).unwrap())
            })
        }else{None};
        let scope=if precise.is_some(){"exact column declaration"}else if rule_binding.is_some(){"effective rule/column and authority dependency closure"}else if selected.is_empty(){"whole-source fallback: unlocated/unknown subject"}else{"source declaration and dependency/writer closure (conservative)"};
        let fingerprint=if let Some(bytes)=precise.or(rule_binding){hash(&bytes)}else{
            // Forward names include helper bodies and referenced schemas.
            // Reverse writers include newly added mutators of a trusted grant.
            loop{
                let before=selected.clone();
                let names=selected.iter().map(|i|decls[*i].name.as_str()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
                let dependencies=selected.iter().flat_map(|i|decls[*i].tokens.iter().filter(|(k,_)|k=="Word").map(|(_,v)|v.as_str())).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
                for (i,d) in decls.iter().enumerate(){
                    let module=std::path::Path::new(&d.file).file_stem().and_then(|s|s.to_str()).unwrap_or("");
                    let operation=if module=="schema"{d.name.clone()}else{format!("{module}.{}",d.name)};
                    let writer=matches!(d.kind.as_str(),"mutation"|"job")&&bound.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==operation).is_some_and(|p|snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(table,_)|names.contains(table.as_str())));
                    let intent=d.kind=="invariant"&&names.contains(d.name.as_str());
                    if dependencies.contains(d.name.as_str())||writer||intent{selected.insert(i);}
                }
                if selected==before{break;}
            }
            if selected.is_empty(){
                // Unknown source ownership is not an empty authority binding.
                hash(&serde_json::to_vec(sources).unwrap())
            }else{
                let mut binding=selected.iter().map(|i|{let d=&decls[*i];(&d.file,&d.kind,&d.name,&d.tokens)}).collect::<Vec<_>>();binding.sort();
                hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&binding).unwrap())
            }
        };
        (key,fingerprint,scope.into())
    }).collect()
}

#[cfg(test)]mod tests{
use super::*;
use std::collections::BTreeSet;
fn note(line:u32,code:&str,message:&str)->snapback4_lang::Diagnostic{snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&snapback4_lang::Site::new("schema.q",line,3),code,message,"review")}
fn ids(source:&str,notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic])->Vec<(String,String,String)>{identities(notes,&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())])}
const SOURCE:&str="table docs:\n  photo: image <=10000 ?\n  title: text <=100\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(photo:image <=10000 ?):\n  return insert docs {photo,title:'x'}\nquery unrelated():\n  return true\n";
#[test]fn independent_edits_and_comments_preserve_column_decisions(){
 let n=note(2,"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE","docs.photo permits omitted or empty media evidence");let a=ids(SOURCE,&[n.clone()]);
 let changed=SOURCE.replace("title: text <=100","title: text <=200").replace("return true","return false");assert_eq!(a,ids(&changed,&[n.clone()]));
 let shifted=format!("-- a comment\n\n{SOURCE}");assert_eq!(a,ids(&shifted,&[note(4,&n.code,&n.message)]));
 let changed=SOURCE.replace("photo: image <=10000 ?","photo: image <=20000 ?");assert_ne!(a[0].1,ids(&changed,&[n])[0].1);
}
#[test]fn flow_decisions_bind_helpers_and_new_writers_but_not_independent_queries(){
 let n=note(8,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","add: optional photo creates docs.photo");let a=ids(SOURCE,&[n.clone()]);
 assert_eq!(a,ids(&SOURCE.replace("return true","return false"),&[n.clone()]));
 let changed=format!("{SOURCE}mutation clear(doc:docs):\n  update docs[doc] {{photo:null}}\n");assert_ne!(a[0].1,ids(&changed,&[n.clone()])[0].1);
 let changed=SOURCE.replace("title:'x'","title:'y'");assert_ne!(a[0].1,ids(&changed,&[n])[0].1);
}
#[test]fn repeated_writes_keep_individual_ids_across_line_shifts(){
 let mut a=note(8,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","add: optional photo creates docs.photo");let mut b=a.clone();b.site.line=9;
 let original=ids(SOURCE,&[a.clone(),b.clone()]);assert_ne!(original[0].0,original[1].0);
 a.site.line+=2;b.site.line+=2;assert_eq!(original,ids(&format!("-- comment\n\n{SOURCE}"),&[a,b]));
}
#[test]fn helpers_that_write_a_trusted_table_are_reverse_dependencies(){
 let s=SOURCE.to_owned()+"fn patch(doc:docs,title:text <=100):\n  update docs[doc] {title}\n  return null\nmutation edit(doc:docs):\n  return patch(doc,'a')\n";
 let n=note(8,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","add: optional photo creates docs.photo");
 assert_ne!(ids(&s,&[n.clone()])[0].1,ids(&s.replace("patch(doc,'a')","patch(doc,'b')"),&[n])[0].1);
}
#[test]fn grouped_column_questions_bind_the_nonfirst_column(){
 let s="table docs:\n  a:text <=100\n  b:text <=100\n  read <- allow\n  online only\n";
 let n=note(2,"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ","docs.a read: protected columns [a, b] admit staff for every state");
 assert_ne!(ids(s,&[n.clone()])[0].1,ids(&s.replace("b:text <=100","b:text <=200"),&[n])[0].1);
}

//! CLI security reporting; compile/test evidence share one analyzer.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#19-wave-28-output-and-witness-decisions-dx11 — gates lead; advisory questions are opt-in.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::security::{self,Report};
mod http;
mod record;
mod presence;
pub(super) mod witnesses;
#[cfg(test)] mod derived_tests;
pub(super) use presence::remember as remember_presence;
pub(super) use record::{remember,capture};
pub(super) fn unauthenticated(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    match http::run(project,bound,sources){Ok(_)=>true,Err(why)=>{eprintln!("security HTTP failure: {why}");false}}
}
pub(super) fn report(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],options:&Options)->Report{
    let augmented=if options.flags.contains_key("security-deploy"){security::derived::augmented(sources,bound)}else{bound.clone()};
    let bound=&augmented;
    let mut selection=options.clone();
    selection.json=true; // Internal fixture loading must not print a second check transcript.
    let selected_fixture=record::selected_fixture(project,options);
    if selected_fixture.is_dir(){selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),selected_fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    let inputs=match selection.flags.get("fixture"){
        Some(path)=>match super::test_fixture::snapshot(std::path::Path::new(path)){Ok(snapshot)=>Some(snapshot),Err(why)=>{let mut report=security::analyze_rules(bound);security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);report.campaign_coverage.insert(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));return report;}},None=>None,
    };
    if let Some(snapshot)=&inputs{selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),snapshot.0.to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    let cached=record::matching(project,&selection,sources);
    let known_failure=cached.as_ref().is_some_and(|r|!r.violations.is_empty())&&!options.flags.contains_key("security-full");
    let mut campaign=if options.flags.contains_key("security-full"){security::challenges::Campaign::default()}else{if options.flags.contains_key("security-deploy"){let mut campaign=security::challenges::Campaign::bounded(10000);campaign.work_remaining=Some(8192);campaign}else{security::challenges::Campaign::bounded(1500)}};
    if known_failure {campaign.work_remaining=Some(0);campaign.unsupported.insert("matching full-test refutations retained; bounded resampling skipped; repair and rerun snapback4 test".into());}
    // An explicitly selected fixture remains the initial-state assumption.
    // Static generation runs only on the fixture-free path; both share this
    // one bounded campaign with all subsequent concrete exploration.
    let mut report=if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture")||known_failure||options.flags.contains_key("security-deploy"){
        security::analyze_rules(bound)
    }else{security::analyze_with_campaign(bound,&mut campaign)};
    security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);
    if !bound.invariants.is_empty()&&!known_failure{
        if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture"){
            match super::test_fixture::engine_at(project,bound,&selection,1_800_000_000_000){
                Ok(fixture)=>if bound.schema.tables.keys().any(|t|fixture.store().rows(t).is_ok_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())){security::challenges::fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,&mut campaign)}else{
                    // An empty starter directory selects generation, just as
                    // no fixture does. Give the direct contradiction its turn
                    // before empty-start mutation permutations spend the cap.
                    report=security::analyze_with_campaign(bound,&mut campaign);
                    security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);
                    security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false)
                },
                Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));}
            }
        }else{security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false);}
    }
    let sites=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&!campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.invariant_site==v.invariant.site.to_string()&&c.reachability.is_reachable())).map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect();
    let campaign=security::challenges::run_selected_after(bound,Some(&sites),campaign);
    security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&campaign);
    report.notes.extend(presence::notes(project,bound,None,sources).unwrap_or_else(|why|vec![snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING",why,"repair the unreadable generated presence baseline").guide("security")]));
    if let Some(record)=cached {security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&security::challenges::Campaign{violations:record.violations,..Default::default()});}
    let mut canonical=security::analyze_rules(bound);
    security::add_source_notes(&mut canonical,sources,bound);
    canonical.notes.extend(report.notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code=="N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING").cloned());
    report.notes=canonical.notes;
    report
}
pub fn audit(options:&Options)->i32{
    let project=project_or_exit();
    let sources=match project.sources(){Ok(s)=>s,Err(e)=>{eprintln!("audit: {e}");return 2;}};
    let bound=compiled_or_exit(snapback4_lang::compile(&sources),options.json);
    // Audit is a permission map plus bounded evidence; `test` owns full campaigns.
    let report=report(&project,&bound,&sources,options);
    let dispositions=match super::security_reviews::review(&project,&sources,&report.notes,None){Ok(d)=>d,Err(error)=>{eprintln!("audit: E_SECURITY_NOTES: {error}");return 2;}};
    if options.json {let mut value=serde_json::to_value(&report).unwrap();value["securityReviews"]=serde_json::to_value(&dispositions).unwrap();println!("{value}");}
    else {let mut human=report.clone();human.notes.retain(|n|crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review);print!("{}",human.text_with_all(options.flags.contains_key("all")));super::security_reviews::print(&dispositions,&report.notes);}
    if report.refuted(){1}else{0}
}

    let broad=report.exposures.iter().filter(|e|e.effects.get("read").is_some_and(|r|r=="allow"||r.starts_with("public"))).count();
